000000r 2               CLK_CPS         = 3579545   ; ~3.58 MHz
000000r 2               CLK_CPMS        = (CLK_CPS / 1000) + 1
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; ***  ONBOARD SERIAL ADAPTER, 65C51  ***
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ROCKWELL_ACIA   = 1
000000r 2               ACIA_USE_VIA_TIMER = 0
000000r 2               
000000r 2               SR_2400         = $0A
000000r 2               SR_4800         = $0C
000000r 2               SR_9600         = $0E
000000r 2               SR_19200        = $0F
000000r 2               SR_115200       = $00
000000r 2               
000000r 2               SR_SELECT       = SR_19200
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .if SR_SELECT = SR_2400
000000r 2               SERIAL_RATE     = 2400
000000r 2               .elseif SR_SELECT = SR_4800
000000r 2               SERIAL_RATE     = 4800
000000r 2               .elseif SR_SELECT = SR_9600
000000r 2               SERIAL_RATE     = 9600
000000r 2               .elseif SR_SELECT = SR_19200
000000r 2               SERIAL_RATE     = 19200
000000r 2               .else
000000r 2               SERIAL_RATE     = 115200
000000r 2               .endif
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .if ROCKWELL_ACIA = 0
000000r 2                   .if ACIA_USE_VIA_TIMER = 0
000000r 2               SWT_INNER_LOOP_CYCLES = 5
000000r 2               BITS_PER_CHAR   = 10          ; 8 + start + stop.
000000r 2               SWT             = ((((CLK_CPS / SERIAL_RATE) + 1) * BITS_PER_CHAR) / SWT_INNER_LOOP_CYCLES) + 1
000000r 2                   .else
000000r 2               BITS_PER_CHAR   = 10          ; 8 + start + stop.
000000r 2               HWT_OVERHEAD    = 50
000000r 2               SWT             = (((CLK_CPS / SERIAL_RATE) + 1) * BITS_PER_CHAR) - HWT_OVERHEAD
000000r 2                   .endif
000000r 2               
000000r 2               SWT_SELECT_L    = SWT .MOD 256
000000r 2               SWT_SELECT_H    = SWT / 256
000000r 2               .endif
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; ***  END OF ONBOARD SERIAL ADAPTER  ***

000000r 2               ; SPI DATA BITS
000000r 2               SPI_BIT_CLK     = 1     ; bit 0, so INC/DEC cycle the clock
000000r 2               SPI_BIT_CSB     = 2     ; bit 1
000000r 2               SPI_BIT_MOSI    = 4     ; bit 2
000000r 2               SPI_BIT_CS_1    = 8     ; bit 3
000000r 2               SPI_BIT_CS_2    = $10   ; bit 4
000000r 2               SPI_BIT_CS_4    = $20   ; bit 5
000000r 2               SPI_BIT_CS_8    = $40   ; bit 6
000000r 2               SPI_BIT_MISO    = $80   ; bit 7, so BIT opcode stores MISO in N
000000r 2               
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_0       = 0
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_1       = SPI_BIT_CS_1
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_2       = SPI_BIT_CS_2
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_3       = SPI_BIT_CS_2 | SPI_BIT_CS_1
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_4       = SPI_BIT_CS_4
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_5       = SPI_BIT_CS_4 | SPI_BIT_CS_1
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_6       = SPI_BIT_CS_4 | SPI_BIT_CS_2
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_7       = SPI_BIT_CS_4 | SPI_BIT_CS_2 | SPI_BIT_CS_1
000000r 2               
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_8       = SPI_BIT_CS_8 | SPI_DEV_0
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_9       = SPI_BIT_CS_8 | SPI_DEV_1
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_A       = SPI_BIT_CS_8 | SPI_DEV_2
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_B       = SPI_BIT_CS_8 | SPI_DEV_3
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_C       = SPI_BIT_CS_8 | SPI_DEV_4
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_D       = SPI_BIT_CS_8 | SPI_DEV_5
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_E       = SPI_BIT_CS_8 | SPI_DEV_6
000000r 2               SPI_DEV_F       = SPI_BIT_CS_8 | SPI_DEV_7
000000r 2               
000000r 2               SPI_DDR_BITS    = SPI_BIT_CLK | SPI_BIT_CSB | SPI_BIT_MOSI | SPI_DEV_F
000000r 2

000000r 2               ; write a byte in A to the IO PORT
000000r 2               .macro IO_PORT_WRITE    port, byte, imm
000000r 2               .ifnblank       imm
000000r 2                               lda     #imm
000000r 2               .endif
000000r 2               .ifblank        byte
000000r 2                               sta     port
000000r 2               .else
000000r 2                               sta     IO_PORT_BYTE port, byte
000000r 2               .endif
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; read a byte into A from the IO PORT/Byte (PORT_N | BYTE_M)
000000r 2               .macro IO_PORT_READ     port, byte
000000r 2               .ifblank        byte
000000r 2                               lda     port
000000r 2               .else
000000r 2                               lda     IO_PORT_BYTE port, byte
000000r 2               .endif
000000r 2               .endmacro

000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ; SPI
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               .macro SPI_SEND_CMD b0, b1, b2, b3, b4, crc
000000r 2                               lda             #b0 | $40
000000r 2                               jsr             SPI_SEND
000000r 2                               lda             #b1
000000r 2                               jsr             SPI_TRANSCEIVE
000000r 2                               sta             ZP_TEMP_2
000000r 2                               lda             #b2
000000r 2                               jsr             SPI_TRANSCEIVE
000000r 2                               lda             #b3
000000r 2                               jsr             SPI_TRANSCEIVE
000000r 2                               lda             #b4
000000r 2                               jsr             SPI_TRANSCEIVE
000000r 2               .ifnblank       crc
000000r 2                               lda             #(crc << 1)+1
000000r 2               .else
000000r 2                               lda             #$FF
000000r 2               .endif
000000r 2                               jsr             SPI_TRANSCEIVE
000000r 2                               jsr             SPI_RECV
000000r 2                               sta             ZP_TEMP
000000r 2               .endmacro

000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               .define LOADA(arg)      lda arg
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro  LDA_CORA    CharOrAddr
000000r 2               .ifnblank   CharOrAddr
000000r 2                               LOADA           CharOrAddr
000000r 2               .endif
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro META_CALL    call, C1, C2, C3, C4, C5, C6, C7, C8, C9
000000r 2                   .ifblank C2
000000r 2                               LDA_CORA        {C1}
000000r 2                               jsr             call
000000r 2                               .exitmacro
000000r 2                   .else
000000r 2                               lda             C1
000000r 2                               jsr             call
000000r 2                   .endif
000000r 2                               META_CALL       {call}, {C2}, {C3}, {C4}, {C5}, {C6}, {C7}, {C8}, {C9}
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro META_JMP     call, C1, C2, C3, C4, C5, C6, C7, C8, C9
000000r 2                   .ifblank C2
000000r 2                               LDA_CORA        {C1}
000000r 2                               jmp             call
000000r 2                               .exitmacro
000000r 2                   .else
000000r 2                               lda             C1
000000r 2                               jsr             call
000000r 2                   .endif
000000r 2                               META_JMP        {call}, {C2}, {C3}, {C4}, {C5}, {C6}, {C7}, {C8}, {C9}
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro  PRINT_CHAR      C1, C2, C3, C4, C5, C6, C7, C8, C9
000000r 2                               META_CALL       WRITE_CHAR, {C1}, {C2}, {C3}, {C4}, {C5}, {C6}, {C7}, {C8}, {C9}
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro  PRINT_CHAR_JMP  C1, C2, C3, C4, C5, C6, C7, C8, C9
000000r 2                               META_JMP        WRITE_CHAR, {C1}, {C2}, {C3}, {C4}, {C5}, {C6}, {C7}, {C8}, {C9}
000000r 2               .endmacro

000023r 2               SERIAL_INIT:
000023r 2  78                           sei
000024r 2  A9 1F                        lda             #$10 | SR_SELECT    ; 8-N-1
000026r 2  8D rr rr                     sta             ACIA_R_CTRL
000029r 2               .if ROCKWELL_ACIA = 1
000029r 2  A9 05                        lda             #ACIA_CMD_BIT_DTRL | ACIA_CMD_BIT_TLIE  ; No parity, no echo, tx & rx interrupts.
00002Br 2               .else
00002Br 2                               lda             #ACIA_CMD_BIT_DTRL | ACIA_CMD_BIT_TLID  ; No parity, no echo, rx interrupts.
00002Br 2               .endif
00002Br 2  8D rr rr                     sta             ACIA_R_CMD
00002Er 2               .if ROCKWELL_ACIA = 1 .OR ACIA_USE_VIA_TIMER = 1
00002Er 2  A9 00                        lda             #SER_SEND_STATUS_READY
000030r 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_SER_SEND_STATUS
000032r 2               .endif
000032r 2  A2 06                        ldx             #IRQ_NUMBER_ONBOARD_SERIAL
000034r 2  A9 rr                        lda             #<SERIAL_IRQ_HANDLER
000036r 2  A0 rr                        ldy             #>SERIAL_IRQ_HANDLER
000038r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             IRQ_SET_VECTOR
00003Br 2  58                           cli
00003Cr 2  60                           rts
00003Dr 2               
00003Dr 2               ; Input a character from the serial interface.
00003Dr 2               ; On return, carry flag indicates whether a key was pressed
00003Dr 2               ; If a key was pressed, the key value will be in the A register
00003Dr 2               ;
00003Dr 2               ; Modifies: flags, A
00003Dr 2               ; TODO: select the appropriate read stream for the current task
00003Dr 2               READ_CHAR_NE:                                         ; no echo entry
00003Dr 2  A9 01                        lda   #1
00003Fr 2  85 rr                        sta   ZP_TEMP
000041r 2  80 02                        bra   SERIAL_READ
000043r 2               READ_CHAR:
000043r 2  64 rr                        stz   ZP_TEMP
000045r 2               SERIAL_READ:
000045r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             BUFFER_SIZE
000048r 2  D0 02                        bne   :+
00004Ar 2  18                           clc
00004Br 2  60                           rts
00004Cr 2               :
00004Cr 2  DA                           phx
00004Dr 2  A6 rr                        ldx             ZP_READ_PTR
00004Fr 2  BD rr rr                     lda             INPUT_BUFFER,X
000052r 2  E6 rr                        inc             ZP_READ_PTR
000054r 2  A6 rr                        ldx             ZP_TEMP
000056r 2  D0 03                        bne    :+                               ; no echo
000058r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr WRITE_CHAR                          ; echo
00005Br 2  FA           :               plx
00005Cr 2  38                           sec
00005Dr 2  60                           rts
00005Er 2               
00005Er 2               ; Write decimal value of .A to output
00005Er 2               WRITE_DEC:
00005Er 2  C9 00                        cmp             #0
000060r 2  10 18                        bpl             WRITE_DEC_U
000062r 2  48                           pha
000063r 2  A9 2D 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR      #'-'
000067r 2  rr           
000068r 2  68                           pla
000069r 2  C9 80                        cmp             #$80                        ; special case for -128
00006Br 2  D0 0A                        bne             :+
00006Dr 2  A9 31 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR      #'1'
000071r 2  rr           
000072r 2  A9 28 4C rr                  PRINT_BYTE_JMP  #$28
000076r 2  rr           
000077r 2               :
000077r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             NEGATE
00007Ar 2               
00007Ar 2               WRITE_DEC_U:
00007Ar 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             MOD_10
00007Dr 2  E0 00                        cpx             #0
00007Fr 2  F0 1B                        beq             :++++
000081r 2  5A                           phy
000082r 2  A8                           tay
000083r 2  8A                           txa
000084r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             MOD_10
000087r 2  E0 00                        cpx             #0
000089r 2  D0 06                        bne             :+
00008Br 2  C9 00                        cmp             #0
00008Dr 2  D0 08                        bne             :++
00008Fr 2  80 09                        bra             :+++
000091r 2               :
000091r 2  48                           pha
000092r 2  8A                           txa
000093r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             WRITE_HEX
000096r 2  68                           pla
000097r 2               :
000097r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             WRITE_HEX
00009Ar 2               :
00009Ar 2  98                           tya
00009Br 2  7A                           ply
00009Cr 2               :
00009Cr 2  4C rr rr                     jmp             WRITE_HEX
00009Fr 2               
00009Fr 2               WRITE_BYTE_MIN:
00009Fr 2  C9 10                        cmp             #$10
0000A1r 2  90 0B                        bcc             WRITE_HEX
0000A3r 2               
0000A3r 2               WRITE_BYTE:
0000A3r 2  48                           pha                                         ; Save A for LSD.
0000A4r 2  4A                           lsr
0000A5r 2  4A                           lsr
0000A6r 2  4A                           lsr
0000A7r 2  4A                           lsr                                         ; MSD to LSD position.
0000A8r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             WRITE_HEX                   ; Output hex digit.
0000ABr 2  68                           pla                                         ; Restore A.
0000ACr 2               
0000ACr 2               WRITE_HEX_MASK:
0000ACr 2  29 0F                        and             #$0F                        ; Mask LSD for hex print.
0000AEr 2               
0000AEr 2               WRITE_HEX:
0000AEr 2  DA                           phx
0000AFr 2  AA                           tax
0000B0r 2  BD rr rr                     lda             HEX_MAP,x
0000B3r 2  22                           .byte           $22                        ; skip a byte (over phx below)
0000B4r 2               
0000B4r 2               ; Output a character (from the A register) to the serial interface.
0000B4r 2               ;
0000B4r 2               ; Modifies: flags
0000B4r 2               ; TODO: select appropriate output stream for the given task
0000B4r 2               WRITE_CHAR:
0000B4r 2               SERIAL_WRITE:
0000B4r 2  DA                           phx
0000B5r 2               
0000B5r 2               .if ROCKWELL_ACIA = 0
0000B5r 2                               phy
0000B5r 2               .endif
0000B5r 2               WRITE_DELAY:
0000B5r 2               .if ROCKWELL_ACIA = 1 .OR ACIA_USE_VIA_TIMER = 1
0000B5r 2  A6 rr                        ldx             ZP_SER_SEND_STATUS
0000B7r 2  E0 00                        cpx             #SER_SEND_STATUS_READY
0000B9r 2  F0 03                        beq             :+
0000BBr 2  CB                           wai                                         ; Leave this in, even if RDY has a pull-up
0000BCr 2  80 F7                        bra             WRITE_DELAY
0000BEr 2               :
0000BEr 2               .endif
0000BEr 2  8D rr rr                     IO_PORT_WRITE   ACIA_R_DATA
0000C1r 2               
0000C1r 2               .if ROCKWELL_ACIA = 1 .OR ACIA_USE_VIA_TIMER = 1
0000C1r 2  A2 01                        ldx             #SER_SEND_STATUS_BUSY
0000C3r 2  86 rr                        stx             ZP_SER_SEND_STATUS
0000C5r 2               .endif
0000C5r 2               
0000C5r 2               .if ROCKWELL_ACIA = 0
0000C5r 2                   .if ACIA_USE_VIA_TIMER = 1
0000C5r 2                               pha
0000C5r 2                               lda             #SWT_SELECT_L
0000C5r 2                               ldy             #SWT_SELECT_H
0000C5r 2                               jsr             VIA_START_T2
0000C5r 2                               pla
0000C5r 2                   .else
0000C5r 2                               ldx             #SWT_SELECT_L + 1
0000C5r 2                               ldy             #SWT_SELECT_H + 1
0000C5r 2               :
0000C5r 2                               dex
0000C5r 2                               bne             :-
0000C5r 2                               dey
0000C5r 2                               bne             :-
0000C5r 2                   .endif
0000C5r 2                               ply
0000C5r 2               .endif
0000C5r 2               
0000C5r 2  FA                           plx
0000C6r 2  60                           rts
0000C7r 2               
0000C7r 2               ; Convenience method to write CR/LF to output stream
0000C7r 2               WRITE_CRLF:
0000C7r 2  A9 0D 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR_JMP  #$0D, #$0A
0000CBr 2  rr A9 0A 4C  
0000CFr 2  rr rr        
0000D1r 2               
0000D1r 2               WRITE_PROMPT:
0000D1r 2  20 rr rr                     PRINT_CRLF
0000D4r 2  A9 54 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR      #'T'
0000D8r 2  rr           
0000D9r 2  AD F0 FF 20                  PRINT_HEX_MASK  $FFF0
0000DDr 2  rr rr        
0000DFr 2  A9 20 20 rr                  PRINT_SPACE
0000E3r 2  rr           
0000E4r 2  A5 rr 20 rr                  PRINT_BYTE      RAM_BANK_REG
0000E8r 2  rr           
0000E9r 2  C9 F0                        cmp             #$F0
0000EBr 2  90 10                        bcc             @not_shared
0000EDr 2  A9 28 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR      #'('
0000F1r 2  rr           
0000F2r 2  AD F1 FF 20                  PRINT_HEX_MASK  $FFF1                       ; Shared RAM sub-bank
0000F6r 2  rr rr        
0000F8r 2  A9 29 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR      #')'
0000FCr 2  rr           
0000FDr 2               
0000FDr 2               @not_shared:
0000FDr 2  A9 3A 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR      #':'
000101r 2  rr           
000102r 2  A5 rr 20 rr                  PRINT_BYTE      ROM_BANK_REG
000106r 2  rr           
000107r 2  A9 3E 4C rr                  PRINT_CHAR_JMP  #'>'
00010Br 2  rr           
00010Cr 2               
00010Cr 2               ; .A, .Y hold the addr of HString to write
00010Cr 2               ; Clobbers .A, .Y; Preserves .X
00010Cr 2               WRITE_HSTRING:
00010Cr 2  DA                           phx
00010Dr 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_HS_TEMP
00010Fr 2  84 rr                        sty             ZP_HS_TEMP + 1
000111r 2  B2 rr                        lda             (ZP_HS_TEMP)                ; Length of HString
000113r 2  AA                           tax
000114r 2  A0 00                        ldy             #0
000116r 2               @write_loop:
000116r 2  C8                           iny
000117r 2  B1 rr 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR      {(ZP_HS_TEMP),Y}
00011Br 2  rr           
00011Cr 2  CA                           dex
00011Dr 2  D0 F7                        bne             @write_loop
00011Fr 2  FA                           plx
000120r 2  60                           rts
000121r 2               
000121r 2               ; Initialize the circular input buffer
000121r 2               ; Modifies: flags, A
000121r 2               INIT_BUFFER:
000121r 2  A5 rr                        lda           ZP_READ_PTR
000123r 2  85 rr                        sta           ZP_WRITE_PTR
000125r 2  60                           rts
000126r 2               
000126r 2               ; Escape sequences
000126r 2               CLEAR_SCR:
000126r 2  A9 1B 20 rr                  PRINT_ESC_SEQ #'[', #'2', #'J'
00012Ar 2  rr A9 5B 20  
00012Er 2  rr rr A9 32  
00013Ar 2  A9 1B 20 rr                  PRINT_ESC_SEQ_JMP #'[', #'0', #';', #'0', #'f'
00013Er 2  rr A9 5B 20  
000142r 2  rr rr A9 30  
000158r 2               
000158r 2               ; Set up the SPI interface registers on the VIA
000158r 2               SPI_INIT:
000158r 2  48                           pha
000159r 2  A9 00 8D rr                  IO_PORT_WRITE   VIA_R_AUX_CTRL, , 0
00015Dr 2  rr           
00015Er 2  8D rr rr                     IO_PORT_WRITE   VIA_R_INT_ENABLE
000161r 2  A9 FF 8D rr                  IO_PORT_WRITE   VIA_R_PER_CTRL, , $FF
000165r 2  rr           
000166r 2  A9 7F 8D rr                  IO_PORT_WRITE   IOR_SPI_DDR,  , SPI_DDR_BITS
00016Ar 2  rr           
00016Br 2  A9 02 8D rr                  IO_PORT_WRITE   IOR_SPI_DATA, , SPI_BIT_CSB   ; de-select all SPI devices
00016Fr 2  rr           
000170r 2  68                           pla
000171r 2  60                           rts
000172r 2               
000172r 2               ; Macro to remove essentially duplicate code
000172r 2               .macro          SPI_SEND_SETUP  mode
000172r 2                               sta             ZP_SPI_DATA_OUT
000172r 2                               phy
000172r 2                               txa
000172r 2                               ora             #SPI_BIT_MOSI
000172r 2                               tay
000172r 2                               lda             ZP_SPI_DATA_OUT
000172r 2                               sei
000172r 2               .ifblank        mode
000172r 2                               asl             ZP_SPI_DATA_IN
000172r 2               .endif
000172r 2                               sec
000172r 2                               rol
000172r 2               .endmacro
000172r 2               
000172r 2               ; Write and Read SPI data
000172r 2               ; Uses two ZP registers for data_in and data_out
000172r 2               ; A: data to send
000172r 2               ; X: device ID to send to/receive from
000172r 2               ; Returns input data in A
000172r 2               ; Modifies A, ZP_SPI_DATA_IN, ZP_SPI_DATA_OUT
000172r 2               SPI_TRANSCEIVE:
000172r 2  85 rr 5A 8A                  SPI_SEND_SETUP
000176r 2  09 04 A8 A5  
00017Ar 2  rr 78 06 rr  
000180r 2  B0 05                        bcs             @spi_send_1
000182r 2               @spi_send_0:
000182r 2  8E rr rr                     stx             IOR_SPI_DATA
000185r 2  80 03                        bra             @spi_send
000187r 2               @spi_send_1:
000187r 2  8C rr rr                     sty             IOR_SPI_DATA
00018Ar 2               @spi_send:
00018Ar 2  EE rr rr                     inc             IOR_SPI_DATA        ; SPI_CLK = 1
00018Dr 2  2C rr rr                     bit             IOR_SPI_DATA        ; MISO (bit 7) => N flag
000190r 2  10 02                        bpl             @spi_recv
000192r 2  E6 rr                        inc             ZP_SPI_DATA_IN      ; incoming bit was a 1 (set LSb = 1)
000194r 2               @spi_recv:
000194r 2  0A                           asl
000195r 2  F0 0A                        beq             SPI_OPERATION_DONE
000197r 2  B0 04                        bcs             @had_1
000199r 2  06 rr                        asl             ZP_SPI_DATA_IN
00019Br 2  80 E5                        bra             @spi_send_0
00019Dr 2               @had_1:
00019Dr 2  06 rr                        asl             ZP_SPI_DATA_IN
00019Fr 2  80 E6                        bra             @spi_send_1
0001A1r 2               
0001A1r 2               SPI_OPERATION_DONE:
0001A1r 2  A9 02                        lda             #SPI_BIT_CSB        ; de-select all SPI devices
0001A3r 2  0C rr rr                     tsb             IOR_SPI_DATA
0001A6r 2  7A                           ply
0001A7r 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_SPI_DATA_IN      ; load the input for return in A
0001A9r 2  58                           cli
0001AAr 2  60                           rts
0001ABr 2               
0001ABr 2               ; Write SPI data
0001ABr 2               ; Uses two ZP registers for data_in and data_out
0001ABr 2               ; A: data to send
0001ABr 2               ; X: device ID to send to
0001ABr 2               ; Modifies A, ZP_SPI_DATA_OUT
0001ABr 2               SPI_SEND:
0001ABr 2  85 rr 5A 8A                  SPI_SEND_SETUP  1
0001AFr 2  09 04 A8 A5  
0001B3r 2  rr 78 38 2A  
0001B7r 2               @send_loop:
0001B7r 2  B0 05                        bcs             @spi_send_1
0001B9r 2  8E rr rr                     stx             IOR_SPI_DATA
0001BCr 2  80 03                        bra             @spi_send
0001BEr 2               @spi_send_1:
0001BEr 2  8C rr rr                     sty             IOR_SPI_DATA
0001C1r 2               @spi_send:
0001C1r 2  EE rr rr                     inc             IOR_SPI_DATA        ; SPI_CLK = 1
0001C4r 2  0A                           asl
0001C5r 2  D0 F0                        bne             @send_loop
0001C7r 2  4C rr rr                     jmp             SPI_OPERATION_DONE
0001CAr 2               
0001CAr 2               ; Read from the SPI device
0001CAr 2               ; X: device to read from
0001CAr 2               ; Result returned in A
0001CAr 2               SPI_RECV:
0001CAr 2  5A                           phy
0001CBr 2  A0 08                        ldy             #8
0001CDr 2  8A                           txa
0001CEr 2  09 06                        ora             #SPI_BIT_MOSI | SPI_BIT_CSB
0001D0r 2  8D rr rr                     sta             IOR_SPI_DATA        ; Select the device to receive from
0001D3r 2  78                           sei
0001D4r 2               @recv_loop:
0001D4r 2  0A                           asl                                 ; Shift in 0 to LSb of result
0001D5r 2  EE rr rr                     inc             IOR_SPI_DATA
0001D8r 2  2C rr rr                     bit             IOR_SPI_DATA        ; MISO (bit 7) => N flag
0001DBr 2  10 01                        bpl             @spi_recv_2
0001DDr 2                                                                   ; Set LSb = 1
0001DDr 2  1A                           inc
0001DEr 2               @spi_recv_2:
0001DEr 2  88                           dey
0001DFr 2  D0 F3                        bne             @recv_loop
0001E1r 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_SPI_DATA_IN
0001E3r 2  4C rr rr                     jmp             SPI_OPERATION_DONE
0001E6r 2               
0001E6r 2               ; Delay for some number of cycles to ensure SPI device is ready to start working
0001E6r 2               SPI_INIT_DELAY:
0001E6r 2  48                           pha
0001E7r 2  DA                           phx
0001E8r 2  5A                           phy
0001E9r 2  8A                           txa                                         ; set SPI device
0001EAr 2  09 06                        ora             #SPI_BIT_CSB | SPI_BIT_MOSI ; de-select all devices
0001ECr 2  AA                           tax
0001EDr 2  09 01                        ora             #SPI_BIT_CLK
0001EFr 2  A0 50                        ldy             #SPI_INIT_DELAY_CYCLES
0001F1r 2               @loop:
0001F1r 2  8D rr rr                     sta             IOR_SPI_DATA
0001F4r 2  8E rr rr                     stx             IOR_SPI_DATA
0001F7r 2  88                           dey
0001F8r 2  D0 F7                        bne             @loop
0001FAr 2  7A                           ply
0001FBr 2  FA                           plx
0001FCr 2  68                           pla
0001FDr 2  60                           rts
0001FEr 2               
0001FEr 2               ; Return (in A) the number of unread bytes in the circular input buffer as an unsigned byte
0001FEr 2               ; Modifies: flags, A
0001FEr 2               BUFFER_SIZE:
0001FEr 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_WRITE_PTR
000200r 2  38                           sec
000201r 2  E5 rr                        sbc             ZP_READ_PTR
000203r 2  60                           rts
000204r 2               
000204r 2               ; Maskable interrupt request handler; by default, do nothing
000204r 2               IRQ_HANDLER:
000204r 2  40                           rti
000205r 2               
000205r 2               
000205r 2               SERIAL_IRQ_HANDLER:
000205r 2  48                           pha
000206r 2               
000206r 2               .if ROCKWELL_ACIA = 1
000206r 2  A9 10                        lda             #ACIA_STATUS_BIT_TDRE
000208r 2               .endif
000208r 2               
000208r 2  2C rr rr                     bit             ACIA_R_STATUS
00020Br 2  10 19                        bpl             @int_done 	            ; bit 7 not set, so not ACIA IRQ
00020Dr 2               
00020Dr 2               .if ROCKWELL_ACIA = 1
00020Dr 2  F0 0B                        beq             @do_recv                ; if not Tx, then must be Rx
00020Fr 2  A9 00                        lda             #SER_SEND_STATUS_READY
000211r 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_SER_SEND_STATUS
000213r 2               
000213r 2               @check_recv:
000213r 2  A9 08                        lda             #ACIA_STATUS_BIT_RDRF   ; is read register full?
000215r 2  2C rr rr                     bit             ACIA_R_STATUS
000218r 2  F0 0C                        beq             @int_done
00021Ar 2               .endif
00021Ar 2               
00021Ar 2               @do_recv:
00021Ar 2  AD rr rr                     IO_PORT_READ    ACIA_R_DATA
00021Dr 2  DA                           phx
00021Er 2  A6 rr                        ldx             ZP_WRITE_PTR
000220r 2  9D rr rr                     sta             INPUT_BUFFER,X
000223r 2  E6 rr                        inc             ZP_WRITE_PTR
000225r 2  FA                           plx
000226r 2               
000226r 2               @int_done:
000226r 2  68                           pla
000227r 2  40                           rti
000228r 2               
000228r 2               ;; *****************************************************************
000228r 2               .if 0
000228r 2               ; I2C
000228r 2               
000228r 2               I2C_SCL = $01
000228r 2               I2C_SDA = $02
000228r 2               I2C_CTRL_PORT = VIA_R_PORTA
000228r 2               I2C_DATA_PORT = VIA_R_DDRA
000228r 2               
000228r 2               .macro I2C_ON       val
000228r 2                           tay
000228r 2                           lda     #val
000228r 2                           ora     I2C_DATA_PORT
000228r 2                           sta     I2C_DATA_PORT
000228r 2                           tya
000228r 2               .endmacro
000228r 2               
000228r 2               .macro I2C_OFF      val
000228r 2                           tay
000228r 2                           lda     #~val
000228r 2                           and     I2C_DATA_PORT
000228r 2                           sta     I2C_DATA_PORT
000228r 2                           tya
000228r 2               .endmacro
000228r 2               
000228r 2               .macro SDA_LOW
000228r 2                           I2C_OFF I2C_SDA
000228r 2               .endmacro
000228r 2               
000228r 2               .macro SCL_LOW
000228r 2                           I2C_OFF I2C_SCL
000228r 2               .endmacro
000228r 2               
000228r 2               .macro SDA_HIGH
000228r 2                           I2C_ON  I2C_SDA
000228r 2               .endmacro
000228r 2               
000228r 2               .macro SCL_HIGH
000228r 2                           I2C_ON  I2C_SDA
000228r 2               .endmacro
000228r 2               
000228r 2               .macro SCL_PULSE
000228r 2                           inc     I2C_DATA_PORT
000228r 2                           dec     I2C_DATA_PORT
000228r 2               .endmacro
000228r 2               
000228r 2               ; A: Byte to send
000228r 2               ; Return (in A): 1 = SUCCESS, 0 = FAILURE
000228r 2               I2C_SEND:
000228r 2                           ldx     #$00
000228r 2                           stx     I2C_CTRL_PORT
000228r 2                           ldx     #$09
000228r 2               @loop:
000228r 2                           dex
000228r 2                           beq     @ack
000228r 2                           rol
000228r 2                           jsr     I2C_SEND_BIT
000228r 2                           bra    @loop
000228r 2               @ack:
000228r 2                           jsr     I2C_RECV_BIT    ; ack in A, 0 = success
000228r 2                           eor     #$01            ; return 1 on success, 0 on fail
000228r 2               @end:
000228r 2                           rts
000228r 2               
000228r 2               
000228r 2               I2C_RECV:   lda     #$00
000228r 2                           sta     I2C_CTRL_PORT
000228r 2                           pha
000228r 2                           ldx     #$09
000228r 2               @loop:      dex
000228r 2                           beq     @end
000228r 2                           jsr     rec_bit
000228r 2                           ror
000228r 2                           pla
000228r 2                           rol
000228r 2                           pha
000228r 2                           jmp     @loop
000228r 2               @end:
000228r 2                           pla
000228r 2                           rts
000228r 2               
000228r 2               ; A: Bit to send
000228r 2               I2C_SEND_BIT:
000228r 2                           bcc     @send_one
000228r 2                           SDA_LOW
000228r 2                           bra    @clock_out
000228r 2               @send_one:
000228r 2                           SDA_HIGH
000228r 2               
000228r 2               @clock_out:
000228r 2                           SCL_PULSE
000228r 2                           SDA_LOW
000228r 2                           rts
000228r 2               
000228r 2               I2C_RECV_BIT:
000228r 2                           SDA_HIGH
000228r 2                           SCL_HIGH
000228r 2                           lda     I2C_CTRL_PORT
000228r 2                           and     #I2C_SDA
000228r 2                           bne     @is_one
000228r 2                           lda     #$00
000228r 2                           jmp     @end
000228r 2               @is_one:
000228r 2                           lda     #$01
000228r 2               @end:
000228r 2                           SCL_LOW
000228r 2                           SDA_LOW
000228r 2                           rts
000228r 2               
000228r 2               
000228r 2               I2C_START:
000228r 2                           SDA_LOW
000228r 2                           SCL_LOW
000228r 2                           rts
000228r 2               
000228r 2               
000228r 2               I2C_STOP:
000228r 2                           SCL_HIGH
000228r 2                           SDA_HIGH
000228r 2                           rts
000228r 2               
000228r 2               
000228r 2               I2C_ACK:
000228r 2                           pha
000228r 2                           lda     #$00
000228r 2                           jsr     I2C_SEND_BIT
000228r 2                           pla
000228r 2                           rts
000228r 2               
000228r 2               I2C_NACK:
000228r 2                           pha
000228r 2                           lda     #$01
000228r 2                           jsr     I2C_SEND_BIT
000228r 2                           pla
000228r 2                           rts
000228r 2               .endif
000228r 2

0002C4r 2               .endif

0002C7r 2  A9 20                    lda     #VIA_T2_INT_BIT         ; disable the T2 interrupt
0002C9r 2  8D rr rr                 sta     VIA_R_INT_ENABLE
0002CCr 2  80 0A                    bra     :++
0002CEr 2

0002D5r 2                                                           ; Do whatever T1 timer would do
0002D5r 2  AD rr rr                 lda     VIA_R_T1C_L             ; clear the interrupt
0002D8r 2

0002D8r 2  68                       pla
0002D9r 2  40                       rti

0000FAr 2               .macro VECTORS
0000FAr 2                               .word   NMI_HANDLER     ; NMI vector
0000FAr 2                               .word   RESET_ENTRY     ; RESET vector
0000FAr 2                               .word   IRQ_HANDLER     ; IRQ vector
0000FAr 2                               ;       will actually be pulled from the Vector RAM, depending on lowest priority IRQ currently triggered,
0000FAr 2                               ;       or vector for IRQ# set in V[0..3] if none are triggered.  Can trigger S/W IRQs by setting V and then
0000FAr 2                               ;       calling BRK.  S/W IRQ# is $F, so setting V[0..3] to $F and v[4..7] to a different number, you can
0000FAr 2                               ;       have up to 16 unique S/W IRQs.  You can invoke a hardware device IRQ handler in the same way
0000FAr 2               .endmacro
0000FAr 2

000100r 2               
000100r 2               ; 0..3 = even indexes, 4..7 = odd
000100r 2               ; x000: one byte
000100r 2               ; xxx1: two byte
000100r 2               ; xxx0 (!x000): three byte
000100r 2               
000100r 2               ;x1xx = Indexed
000100r 2               ;   x10x = Indexed by X
000100r 2               ;   x11x (!1110) = Indexed by Y
000100r 2               ;1xxx = Indirect (!100x && !1110)
000100r 2               
000100r 2               ; INDEXED SUFFIXES
000100r 2               ; 010x == ,X
000100r 2               ; 110x == ,X)
000100r 2               ; 011x == ,Y
000100r 2               ; 1111 == ),Y
000100r 2               
000100r 2               AM_ACC   := 0     ;                     %0000
000100r 2               AM_REL   := 1     ; $rr => $aaaa        %0001
000100r 2               AM_ZPREL := 2     ; $zz,$rr => $aaaa    %0010
000100r 2               AM_ZP    := 3     ; $zz                 %0011
000100r 2               AM_ABSX  := 4     ; $aaaa,X             %0100
000100r 2               AM_ZPX   := 5     ; $zz,X               %0101
000100r 2               AM_ABSY  := 6     ; $aaaa,Y             %0110
000100r 2               AM_ZPY   := 7     ; $zz,Y               %0111
000100r 2               AM_IMP   := 8     ;                     %1000
000100r 2               AM_IMM   := 9     ; #$ii                %1001
000100r 2               AM_IND   := $A    ; ($aaaa)             %1010
000100r 2               AM_ZPIND := $B    ; ($zz)               %1011
000100r 2               AM_ABSIX := $C    ; ($aaaa,X)           %1100
000100r 2               AM_ZPIX  := $D    ; ($zz,X)             %1101
000100r 2               AM_ABS   := $E    ; $aaaa               %1110
000100r 2               AM_ZPIY  := $F    ; ($zz),Y             %1111
000100r 2               
000100r 2               ; ADDRESSING MODES
000100r 2               ; Even Opcode Modes (%xxxxyyz0), xxxx = row, yy = column, z = 0 (low nibble), z = 1 (hi nibble)
000100r 2               MN_AMODE_EVEN:
000100r 2  88 33 08 EE      .byte $88,$33,$08,$EE	; %0000yyz0
000104r 2  B1 53 08 4E      .byte $B1,$53,$08,$4E	; %0001yyz0
000108r 2  8E 33 08 EE      .byte $8E,$33,$08,$EE	; %0010yyz0
00010Cr 2  B1 55 08 44      .byte $B1,$55,$08,$44	; %0011yyz0
000110r 2  88 38 08 EE      .byte $88,$38,$08,$EE	; %0100yyz0
000114r 2  B1 58 88 48      .byte $B1,$58,$88,$48	; %0101yyz0
000118r 2  88 33 08 EA      .byte $88,$33,$08,$EA	; %0110yyz0
00011Cr 2  B1 55 88 4C      .byte $B1,$55,$88,$4C	; %0111yyz0
000120r 2  81 33 88 EE      .byte $81,$33,$88,$EE	; %1000yyz0
000124r 2  B1 75 88 4E      .byte $B1,$75,$88,$4E	; %1001yyz0
000128r 2  99 33 88 EE      .byte $99,$33,$88,$EE	; %1010yyz0
00012Cr 2  B1 75 88 64      .byte $B1,$75,$88,$64	; %1011yyz0
000130r 2  89 33 88 EE      .byte $89,$33,$88,$EE	; %1100yyz0
000134r 2  B1 58 88 48      .byte $B1,$58,$88,$48	; %1101yyz0
000138r 2  89 33 88 EE      .byte $89,$33,$88,$EE	; %1110yyz0
00013Cr 2  B1 58 88 48      .byte $B1,$58,$88,$48	; %1111yyz0
000140r 2

000140r 2  8D 33 89 2E      .byte $8D,$33,$89,$2E	; %xxx0yyz1

000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; ZP_D_XAM, ZP_D_XAM+1: Address to Disassemble
000000r 2               ; .A.Y: Start address of instruction to disassemble
000000r 2               ; C=0 means single instruction, C=1 means number of instructions/bytes to print in .X

00017Cr 2  90 01            bcc         :+
00017Er 2  1A               inc
00017Fr 2               
00017Fr 2               :
00017Fr 2  4C rr rr         PRINT_CHAR_JMP
000182r 2               
000182r 2               _disasm_absolute:
000182r 2  C8               iny
000183r 2  20 rr rr         jsr         _disasm_zeropage
000186r 2  88               dey
000187r 2  80 0B            bra         _disasm_zp_byte

00018Dr 2  rr           
00018Er 2                   ; fall through
00018Er 2

000193r 2  rr
