00019Ar 2               
00019Ar 2               
00019Ar 1               .include "hyforth/hyforth.s"
00019Ar 2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
00019Ar 2               ;
00019Ar 2               ;  Patrick Struthers - March 2026
00019Ar 2               ;      - THANKS to AGSB for the starting point here....
00019Ar 2               ;
00019Ar 2               ;  The HyForth project starts here with AGSB's core Forth engine;
00019Ar 2               ;  the engine now resides in ROM (as of 5/1/26) and the heap is copied to RAM
00019Ar 2               ;  on cold start.
00019Ar 2               ;
00019Ar 2               ;  The ulitmate purpose of working this port through is to develop a
00019Ar 2               ;  flexible and powerful operating system for the Hydra-16 of reasonable
00019Ar 2               ;  efficiency and minimal memory footprint.  Eventually, HyForth will be
00019Ar 2               ;  able to grow and shrink in RAM footprint according to context.
00019Ar 2               ;
00019Ar 2               ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------
00019Ar 2               ;
00019Ar 2               .debuginfo
00019Ar 2               .setcpu "65C02"
00019Ar 2               
00019Ar 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00019Ar 2               ; macros for dictionary, creates code as follows:
00019Ar 2               ;
00019Ar 2               ;   h_name:
00019Ar 2               ;   .word  link_to_previous_entry
00019Ar 2               ;   .byte  strlen(name) + flags
00019Ar 2               ;   .byte  name
00019Ar 2               ;   name:
00019Ar 2               ;
00019Ar 2               ; label for primitives
00019Ar 2               ;
00019Ar 2               .macro makelabel arg1, arg2
00019Ar 2               .ident (.concat (arg1, arg2)):
00019Ar 2               .endmacro
00019Ar 2               ;
00019Ar 2               ; header for primitives
00019Ar 2               ; the entry point for dictionary is h_~name~
00019Ar 2               ; the entry point for code is ~name~
00019Ar 2               ;
00019Ar 2               .macro def_word name, label, flag
00019Ar 2               makelabel "h_", label
00019Ar 2               .ident(.sprintf("H%04X", hcount + 1)):
00019Ar 2                 .word .ident (.sprintf ("H%04X", hcount))
00019Ar 2               hcount .set hcount + 1
00019Ar 2                 .byte .strlen(name) + flag + 0 ; nice trick !
00019Ar 2                 .byte name
00019Ar 2               makelabel "", label
00019Ar 2               .endmacro
00019Ar 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00019Ar 2               ;  macros for PGS stuff
00019Ar 2               ;
00019Ar 2               .macro WCRLF_np                ; no push of A
00019Ar 2                      lda #$0D
00019Ar 2                      jsr WRITE_CHAR
00019Ar 2                      lda #$0A
00019Ar 2                      jsr WRITE_CHAR
00019Ar 2               .endmacro
00019Ar 2               
00019Ar 2               .macro WCRLF
00019Ar 2                      pha
00019Ar 2                      lda #$0D
00019Ar 2                      jsr WRITE_CHAR
00019Ar 2                      lda #$0A
00019Ar 2                      jsr WRITE_CHAR
00019Ar 2                      pla
00019Ar 2               .endmacro
00019Ar 2               
00019Ar 2               .macro  WSEQ_np  strlbl
00019Ar 2                    phy
00019Ar 2                    ldy #0
00019Ar 2               :
00019Ar 2                    lda strlbl, y
00019Ar 2                    beq :+
00019Ar 2                    iny
00019Ar 2                    jsr WRITE_CHAR
00019Ar 2                    bra :-
00019Ar 2               :
00019Ar 2                    WCRLF_np
00019Ar 2                    ply
00019Ar 2               .endmacro
00019Ar 2               
00019Ar 2               
00019Ar 2               .macro  WSEQ_raw  strlbl
00019Ar 2                    phy
00019Ar 2                    ldy #0
00019Ar 2               :
00019Ar 2                    lda strlbl, y
00019Ar 2                    beq :+
00019Ar 2                    iny
00019Ar 2                    jsr WRITE_CHAR
00019Ar 2                    bra :-
00019Ar 2               :
00019Ar 2                    ply
00019Ar 2               .endmacro
00019Ar 2               
00019Ar 2               .macro WSEQ  strlbl
00019Ar 2                     pha
00019Ar 2                     WSEQ_np  strlbl
00019Ar 2                     pla
00019Ar 2               .endmacro
00019Ar 2               ;
00019Ar 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00019Ar 2               ;  for error messages
00019Ar 2               ;
00019Ar 2               .macro ERR_entry errmsg
00019Ar 2                   .byte <errmsg
00019Ar 2                   .byte >errmsg
00019Ar 2                   emcount .set emcount + 1
00019Ar 2               .endmacro
00019Ar 2               
00019Ar 2               .macro WERR errptr
00019Ar 2               ;.macro WERR
00019Ar 2                   .local werrloop, werrend
00019Ar 2                   WCRLF_np
00019Ar 2                   ldy #0
00019Ar 2                   lda (errptr),y
00019Ar 2                   sta TEMP0
00019Ar 2                   iny
00019Ar 2                   lda (errptr),y
00019Ar 2                   sta TEMP0+1
00019Ar 2                   ldy #0
00019Ar 2               werrloop:
00019Ar 2                   lda (TEMP0),y
00019Ar 2                   beq werrend
00019Ar 2                   iny
00019Ar 2                   jsr WRITE_CHAR
00019Ar 2                   bra werrloop
00019Ar 2               werrend:
00019Ar 2                   WCRLF_np
00019Ar 2                   rts
00019Ar 2               .endmacro
00019Ar 2               ;
00019Ar 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00019Ar 2               ;              library list  - handle like an error/token jumptable
00019Ar 2               ;
00019Ar 2               ;                --- this is also how MS-BASIC knows where to send
00019Ar 2               ;   the code when a tokenized command is invoked.  Inherent limitation
00019Ar 2               ;   with one-byte tokens (for errors or libraries) is max of 255 entries.
00019Ar 2               ;
00019Ar 2               .macro LIBENTRY libname
00019Ar 2                   .byte <libname
00019Ar 2                   .byte >libname
00019Ar 2                   libcount .set libcount + 1
00019Ar 2               .endmacro
00019Ar 2               ;
00019Ar 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00019Ar 2               ; variables for macros
00019Ar 2               
00019Ar 2               hcount .set 0
00019Ar 2               emcount .set 0             ; # of error messages set up
00019Ar 2               libcount .set 0
00019Ar 2               
00019Ar 2               H0000 = 0
00019Ar 2               
00019Ar 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00019Ar 2               ;               CONFIGURATION OPTIONS
00019Ar 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00019Ar 2               
00019Ar 2               ; number conversion
00019Ar 2               numbers := 1      ; include DEC/BIN/HEX conversion
00019Ar 2               
00019Ar 2               SINGLE := 1     ; single digits hard coded?
00019Ar 2               
00019Ar 2               DEBUG := 1        ; enable inclusion of debug code
00019Ar 2               
00019Ar 2               HYWORDS := 1      ; add in additional hardcoded words / logic
00019Ar 2               
00019Ar 2               ANSIOK := 1         ; add ANSI screen stuff
00019Ar 2               
00019Ar 2               YSOUND := 1        ; add sound support
00019Ar 2               
00019Ar 2               ;TXT2STACK := 1     ; use old TXTGET instead of new
00019Ar 2               
00019Ar 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00019Ar 2               ;              for PGS hyforth stuff
00019Ar 2               ;
00019Ar 2               ; error codes
00019Ar 2               ;
00019Ar 2               ERR_RPTR := $01    ; return stack pointer error
00019Ar 2               ERR_SPTR := $02    ; data stack pointer error
00019Ar 2               ERR_DIV0 := $03    ; divide by zero
00019Ar 2               ERR_MEM := $04     ; memory not-avail
00019Ar 2               ERR_UKW := $05     ; unknown word
00019Ar 2               ERR_SEC := $06     ; security error, ie dangerous address write
00019Ar 2               ERR_SYS := $07     ; return from system call error
00019Ar 2                 ; warnings
00019Ar 2               WRN_SEC := $86     ; security
00019Ar 2               WRN_MEM := $84     ; memory alloc
00019Ar 2               ;
00019Ar 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00019Ar 2               ;                 CORE ENGINE CONSTANTS
00019Ar 2               ;
00019Ar 2               CELL = 2         ; cell size, two bytes, 16-bit
00019Ar 2               FLAG_IMM = 1<<7  ; immediate flag
00019Ar 2               FLAG_COM = 1<<6  ; compiled flag
00019Ar 2               MAXSTR = 100
00019Ar 2               
00019Ar 2               ; terminal input buffer, forward
00019Ar 2               ; getline, token, skip, scan, depends on page boundary
00019Ar 2               ; INBUF = $0400  (see segment STACKS below)
00019Ar 2               ; moves forwards
00019Ar 2               INBUF_end = $FD
00019Ar 2               
00019Ar 2               ; data stacks
00019Ar 2               ; moves backwards, push decreases before copy
00019Ar 2               DSEND = $7E
00019Ar 2               
00019Ar 2               ; return stack
00019Ar 2               ; moves backwards, push decreases before copy
00019Ar 2               RTEND = $FE
00019Ar 2               
00019Ar 2               ; malloc stack and managed memory area
00019Ar 2               ; both move downwards like other stacks
00019Ar 2               ;
00019Ar 2               MEMEND = $01FE            ; pointers DOWN from MEMEND+MEMSTK (store-and-decrement)
00019Ar 2               MEMTOP = $7BF0            ; malloc allocates DOWN from MEMTOP
00019Ar 2               SCRBUF = $7C00            ; screen buffer 1K going up
00019Ar 2               ;
00019Ar 2               ;   types:  <type> <len0 len1> <data data data...>
00019Ar 2               ;
00019Ar 2               MEM_SCR = $01  ; screen  (1024 bytes, with 63 char rows terminated either $09 or $00
00019Ar 2               MEM_SZ = $04   ; zero-termed string
00019Ar 2               MEM_FRAC = $06 ; 'fractional binary' <type> <03 00> <offset> <xxxx>
00019Ar 2               MEM_TAB = $07  ; table
00019Ar 2               ;
00019Ar 2               ; spi
00019Ar 2               ;F9E8 SPI_INIT_DELAY
00019Ar 2               ;F9CC SPI_RECV
00019Ar 2               ;F9AD SPI_SEND
00019Ar 2               ;F9A3 SPI_OPERATION_DONE
00019Ar 2               ;F974 SPI_TRANSCEIVE
00019Ar 2               
00019Ar 2               INROM = $A000
00019Ar 2               
00019Ar 2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
00019Ar 2               ;       Look closely at hyforth.cfg and the output of ca65/ld65 after
00019Ar 2               ;  a build; the RAM and ROM code is carefully arranged when the binary
00019Ar 2               ;  is created, to make initialization easier and optimize RAM use.
00019Ar 2               ;  The main program engine starts at $EA00 in OS ROM and is only about 500 bytes
00019Ar 2               ;  long.  Additional functions, then initialization and support code follow.
00019Ar 2               ;  The heap or 'dictionary' primitives are stored on a banked ROM, mapped
00019Ar 2               ;  to $A000, along with some meta-compiled words following the primitives.
00019Ar 2               ;
00019Ar 2               ;       Currently, the primitives are loaded with 'COPYTORAM' (at $A000)
00019Ar 2               ;  using parameters for the size and location of the heap stored at $A003,4,5.
00019Ar 2               ;
00019Ar 2               ;       The dictionary has a particular structure, with 'bye' and 'abort'
00019Ar 2               ;  at the beginning; there are some core words,
00019Ar 2               ;  then contents of 'primitives.s', then 'hywords.s', followed by
00019Ar 2               ;  the end of the dictionary with critical items such as 'fetch', 'store',
00019Ar 2               ;  'immediate', 'compile, 'semis', 'exit, and ancillary stuff.  Any sub-
00019Ar 2               ;  routines that are called by multiple words (and therefore need set
00019Ar 2               ;  addresses) are considered 'support code' and reside in OS ROM following
00019Ar 2               ;  the basic HyForth engine.
00019Ar 2               ;
00019Ar 2               ;       HyForth is started by running '800R' or 'EA00R'; it can be supplemented
00019Ar 2               ;  with the meta-compiled words and runtime-loaded binaries by using
00019Ar 2               ;  'autoload' and 'bload' respectively.
00019Ar 2               ;
00019Ar 2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
00019Ar 2               ;                   ZERO PAGE USAGE
00019Ar 2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
00019Ar 2               .segment "ZEROPAGE"
00002Er 2               ;
00002Er 2               .org $B0
0000B0  2               ZPSTART:
0000B0  2               ;                 Utilities, some BIOS crossover
0000B0  2               ;
0000B0  2  xx xx        Z_DEBUG:    .res 2             ; dedicated ZP temp for debugger  $B0
0000B2  2               ;
0000B2  2  xx xx        Z_MATH1:    .res 2             ; ZP temp for math routines   $B2  (*, /, */)
0000B4  2  xx xx        Z_MATH2:    .res 2             ; ZP temp for math routines   $B4  (*, /, */)
0000B6  2  xx xx        Z_MATH3:    .res 2             ; ZP temp for math routines   $B6  (*, /, */)
0000B8  2  xx xx        Z_MATH4:    .res 2             ; ZP temp for math/ANSI       $B8  (HEX2DIG, */)
0000BA  2               ;
0000BA  2  xx xx        Z_ANSI1:    .res 2             ; ZP temp for ANSI            $BA
0000BC  2  xx xx        Z_ANSI2:    .res 2             ; ZP temp for ANSI            $BC
0000BE  2  xx xx        Z_ANSI3:    .res 2             ; ZP temp for ANSI            $BE
0000C0  2               ;
0000C0  2               ;                  HyFedit specific
0000C0  2               ;
0000C0  2  xx xx        HFE_BPTR:   .res 2             ; pointer into 1K buffer      $C0
0000C2  2  xx           HFE_CH:     .res 1             ; cursor horizontal (columns) $C2
0000C3  2  xx           HFE_CV:     .res 1             ; cursor vertical (rows)      $C3
0000C4  2  xx           HFE_T0:     .res 1             ; reserved                    $C4
0000C5  2  xx           HFE_T0H:    .res 1             ; reserved                    $C5
0000C6  2  xx           HFE_T1:     .res 1             ; reserved                    $C6
0000C7  2  xx           HFE_T1H:    .res 1             ; reserved                    $C7
0000C8  2               ;HFE_T2 is at $FE/$FF  (TEMP7)
0000C8  2               ;
0000C8  2               ;                   HyForth setup stuff
0000C8  2               ;
0000C8  2               TEMP0:                    ; DUMPREG, lib/libs    $C8
0000C8  2  xx xx           .res 2
0000CA  2               TEMP8:                        ;  cons and pick   $CA
0000CA  2  xx xx           .res 2
0000CC  2               TEMP9:                        ;   Imm            $CC
0000CC  2  xx xx           .res 2
0000CE  2               MEMPTR:                       ;   malloc         $CE
0000CE  2  xx xx           .res 2
0000D0  2               MEMLAST:
0000D0  2  xx xx           .res 2                      ;  malloc         $D0
0000D2  2               TIB:
0000D2  2  xx xx           .res 2          ; pointer to input buffer     $D2
0000D4  2               TIBEND:
0000D4  2  xx xx           .res 2          ; pointer to end of TIB       $D4
0000D6  2               DFLAG:
0000D6  2  xx              .res 1          ; debug flag                  $D6
0000D7  2               ERRFLAG:
0000D7  2  xx              .res 1          ; error type, 0 = none        $D7
0000D8  2               ERRPTR:
0000D8  2  xx xx           .res 2          ; ptr to mitigation/message   $D8
0000DA  2               DIGBASE:
0000DA  2  xx              .res 1          ; base for number conversion  $DA
0000DB  2               RSEED:
0000DB  2  xx xx xx xx     .res 4          ; random # seed               $DB
0000DF  2               ALFLAG:            ; autoload flag               $DF
0000DF  2  xx              .res 1
0000E0  2               ;
0000E0  2               ;                   internal Forth
0000E0  2               ;
0000E0  2  00 00        STATUS:     .word $0   ; state at lsb, last size+flag at msb   $E0
0000E2  2  00 00        CURBUF:     .word $0   ; CURBUF next free byte in TIB          $E2
0000E4  2  00 00        LASTHEAP:   .word $0   ; last link cell                        $E4
0000E6  2  00 00        NEXTHEAP:   .word $0   ; next free cell in heap dictionary     $E6
0000E8  2               ;
0000E8  2               ;                   pointer registers
0000E8  2               ;
0000E8  2  00 00        DSPTR:      .word $0   ; data stack pointer                    $E8
0000EA  2  00 00        RTPTR:      .word $0   ; return stack pointer                  $EA
0000EC  2  00 00        INSTPTR:    .word $0   ; instruction pointer                   $EC
0000EE  2  00 00        WORKREG:    .word $0   ; working register                      $EE
0000F0  2               ;
0000F0  2               ;                    TEMP1 - 4
0000F0  2               ;
0000F0  2  00 00        TEMP1:    .word $0     ; first                                 $F0
0000F2  2  00 00        TEMP2:    .word $0     ; second                                $F2
0000F4  2  00 00        TEMP3:    .word $0     ; third  (two bytes)                    $F4
0000F6  2  00 00        TEMP4:    .word $0     ; fourth  (two bytes)                   $F6
0000F8  2               ;
0000F8  2               ;          NXTTOK, BACKHEAP, TEMP5 - 7
0000F8  2               ;
0000F8  2  00 00        NXTTOK:     .word $0   ; next token in tib (INBUF)             $F8
0000FA  2  00 00        BACKHEAP:   .word $0   ; hold 'here while compile              $FA
0000FC  2               supprint:              ; suppress printing in MEMCPY
0000FC  2  xx           TEMP5:   .res 1        ;    WORDS, supprint for MEMCPY         $FC
0000FD  2  xx           TEMP6:   .res 1        ;    WORDS, DIGCOUNT, TEXTGET           $FD
0000FE  2               HFE_T2:                ;   third temp variable for HyFedit
0000FE  2  xx xx        TEMP7:   .res 2        ;    AUTOLOAD, HyFedit                  $FE
000100  2               ;
000100  2               ; *** $B0-$FF total usage in ZP, including TEMP vars ***
000100  2               ;
000100  2               ;   HYDRA-16 serial/read buffers at $200 and $300 so skip those
000100  2               ;
000100  2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
000100  2               ;                   FORTH STACKS
000100  2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
000100  2               .segment "BUFFERS"
000100r 2               INBUF:
000100r 2  xx xx xx xx        .res 256
000104r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000108r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000200r 2               DS:                          ; data stack (S)
000200r 2  xx xx xx xx        .res 126
000204r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000208r 2  xx xx xx xx  
00027Er 2  xx xx              .res 2
000280r 2               RT:                          ; return stack (R)
000280r 2  xx xx xx xx        .res 126
000284r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000288r 2  xx xx xx xx  
0002FEr 2  xx xx              .res 2
000300r 2               MEMSTK:                      ; memory manager
000300r 2  xx xx xx xx        .res 510
000304r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000308r 2  xx xx xx xx  
0004FEr 2  xx xx              .res 2
000500r 2               ;
000500r 2               ;
000500r 2               .segment "FORTH_ROM"              ; regular core
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ; ************ the real deal...
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               
000000r 2               forth_main:
000000r 2  4C rr rr         jmp cold
000003r 2  4C rr rr         jmp COPYTORAM
000006r 2               HYPROMPT:
000006r 2  0D 0A            .byte $0D, $0A
000008r 2  48 46 3E         .byte "HF>"
00000Br 2  00               .byte 0
00000Cr 2               ;
00000Cr 2               HYLIBMSG:
00000Cr 2  0D 0A            .byte $0D, $0A
00000Er 2  4C 69 62 61      .byte "Libaries:"
000012r 2  72 69 65 73  
000016r 2  3A           
000017r 2  00               .byte 0
000018r 2               .ifdef DEBUG
000018r 2               WDISP:
000018r 2  0D 0A            .byte $0D, $0A
00001Ar 2  57 3D            .byte "W="
00001Cr 2  00               .byte 0
00001Dr 2               WATDISP:
00001Dr 2  20 20 5B 57      .byte "  [W]="
000021r 2  5D 3D        
000023r 2  00               .byte 0
000024r 2               .endif
000024r 2               ;
000024r 2               ;
000024r 2               ;
000024r 2               cold:
000024r 2  D8               cld
000025r 2  20 rr rr         jsr CLEAR           ; zero out zero page, INBUF, DS, RT, SCRBUF
000028r 2                                       ;
000028r 2               warm:
000028r 2               ; linked list of headers
000028r 2                                               ; initialize HEAP pointers
000028r 2  AD 03 A0         lda INROM+3                 ; standard 'heap' now sitting in ROM
00002Br 2  85 E5            sta LASTHEAP + 1            ; and 'switcher' ('exit') code handles
00002Dr 2                                               ; the rest.
00002Dr 2  AD 04 A0         lda INROM+4
000030r 2  85 E4            sta LASTHEAP
000032r 2                                               ; next heap free cell
000032r 2  AD 05 A0         lda INROM+5                 ; ends stored here in ROM also
000035r 2  1A               inc a
000036r 2  85 E7            sta NEXTHEAP + 1
000038r 2  64 E6            stz NEXTHEAP
00003Ar 2  64 D7            stz ERRFLAG                ; clear ERROR flag
00003Cr 2               
00003Cr 2  A0 rr            ldy #>(MEMSTK+MEMEND)       ; initialize memory manager area
00003Er 2  84 CF            sty MEMPTR + 1
000040r 2  A0 rr            ldy #<(MEMSTK+MEMEND)
000042r 2  84 CE            sty MEMPTR
000044r 2  A0 F0            ldy #<MEMTOP
000046r 2  84 D0            sty MEMLAST
000048r 2  A0 7B            ldy #>MEMTOP
00004Ar 2  84 D1            sty MEMLAST+1
00004Cr 2               
00004Cr 2  A0 00            ldy #<SCRBUF                ; initialize screen pointer
00004Er 2  8C rr rr         sty HFEBUF                  ; HFE_BPTR is set up when
000051r 2  A0 7C            ldy #>SCRBUF                ; HFE_COLD is called
000053r 2  8C rr rr         sty HFEBUF+1                ; done.  SCRBUF already cleared
000056r 2               
000056r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000056r 2               ; various reinitialization points
000056r 2               ;
000056r 2               reset:
000056r 2  A0 FD            ldy #INBUF_end
000058r 2  84 D4            sty TIBEND
00005Ar 2  A0 rr            ldy #<INBUF
00005Cr 2  84 D2            sty TIB
00005Er 2  A0 rr            ldy #>INBUF
000060r 2  84 D3            sty TIB+1
000062r 2  84 D5            sty TIBEND+1
000064r 2  84 E3            sty CURBUF+1
000066r 2  84 F9            sty NXTTOK+1
000068r 2               
000068r 2  A0 rr            ldy #>DS                     ; DS and RT are now half page each
00006Ar 2  84 E9            sty DSPTR + 1
00006Cr 2  A0 rr            ldy #>RT
00006Er 2  84 EB            sty RTPTR + 1
000070r 2               
000070r 2  A9 FF            lda #$FF                       ; DEBUG OFF by default
000072r 2  85 D6            sta DFLAG
000074r 2  64 DF            stz ALFLAG                   ; autoload flag OFF
000076r 2               
000076r 2               abort:                            ; clear DS (clear data stack)
000076r 2  A0 7E            ldy #<DSEND
000078r 2  84 E8            sty DSPTR
00007Ar 2               
00007Ar 2               errrtn:                          ; return from error w/o clearing DS
00007Ar 2               quit:                             ; clear RT
00007Ar 2  A0 FE            ldy #<RTEND
00007Cr 2  84 EA            sty RTPTR
00007Er 2               
00007Er 2  20 rr rr         jsr wrterror                 ; print any error messages
000081r 2  A0 00            ldy #0                       ; reset INBUF
000083r 2  A9 00            lda #0
000085r 2  91 D2            sta (TIB),y                  ; clear INBUF stuff
000087r 2  64 E2            stz CURBUF                   ; clear cursor  (pointer into INBUF)
000089r 2  64 E0            stz STATUS                   ; status is 'interpret' == \0
00008Br 2               
00008Br 2  2C               .byte $2c       ; mask next two bytes, jump to resolve/okey
00008Cr 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00008Cr 2               ; the outer loop
00008Cr 2               
00008Cr 2               resolvept:
00008Cr 2  rr rr            .word okey
00008Er 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00008Er 2               okey:               ; well shit, I hope this is easy....
00008Er 2               resolve:           ; get a token
00008Er 2  20 rr rr         jsr token      ; then just process the regular way
000091r 2               .ifdef DEBUG
000091r 2  A5 D6            lda DFLAG                 ; DEBUG
000093r 2  D0 0F            bne RVPSKIP
000095r 2  A9 0D 20 rr      WCRLF_np
000099r 2  rr A9 0A 20  
00009Dr 2  rr rr        
00009Fr 2  A9 50            lda #'P'
0000A1r 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR            ; DEBUG
0000A4r 2               .endif
0000A4r 2               
0000A4r 2               RVPSKIP:
0000A4r 2               
0000A4r 2               RESFIND:                ; load last or 'latest' word on heap
0000A4r 2  A5 E5            lda LASTHEAP + 1
0000A6r 2  85 F3            sta TEMP2 + 1
0000A8r 2  A5 E4            lda LASTHEAP
0000AAr 2  85 F2            sta TEMP2
0000ACr 2               
0000ACr 2               RESLOOP:              ; lsb linked list
0000ACr 2  A5 F2            lda TEMP2
0000AEr 2  85 EE            sta WORKREG             ; so 'last' -> W
0000B0r 2  05 F3            ora TEMP2+1             ; only zero if both are zero
0000B2r 2  D0 07            bne RESEACH              ; PGS - did he forget this?  Sure did...
0000B4r 2               
0000B4r 2               WORDNOTFOUND:
0000B4r 2  A9 05            lda #ERR_UKW           ; UNKNOWN WORD error
0000B6r 2  85 D7            sta ERRFLAG
0000B8r 2  4C rr rr         jmp errrtn ; end of dictionary, no more words to search, abort
0000BBr 2               
0000BBr 2               RESEACH:                        ; msb linked list
0000BBr 2  A5 F3            lda TEMP2 + 1
0000BDr 2  85 EF            sta WORKREG + 1           ; update next link
0000BFr 2               
0000BFr 2  A2 EE            ldx #WORKREG
0000C1r 2  A0 F2            ldy #TEMP2
0000C3r 2  20 rr rr         jsr copyfrom                  ; W += 2, now pointing at size/flag byte from 'here'
0000C6r 2  A0 00            ldy #0              ; compare words
0000C8r 2  B1 EE            lda (WORKREG), y    ; save the flag, first byte is (size and flag)
0000CAr 2  85 E1            sta STATUS + 1
0000CCr 2                           ;; *** start of mod for bit check
0000CCr 2  29 3F            and #$3F            ; mask off flags
0000CEr 2  38               sec
0000CFr 2  F1 F8            sbc (NXTTOK), y    ; compare lengths
0000D1r 2  D0 D9            bne RESLOOP
0000D3r 2  C8               iny
0000D4r 2               ; compare chars
0000D4r 2               RESEQUAL:
0000D4r 2  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK), y
0000D6r 2  C9 20            cmp #$20            ; space ends
0000D8r 2  F0 09            beq RESDONE
0000DAr 2  38               sec                 ; verify
0000DBr 2  F1 EE            sbc (WORKREG), y
0000DDr 2  0A               asl                 ; clean 7-bit ascii
0000DEr 2  D0 CC            bne RESLOOP
0000E0r 2  C8               iny                 ; get next char
0000E1r 2  D0 F1            bne RESEQUAL
0000E3r 2               
0000E3r 2               RESDONE:
0000E3r 2  98               tya                ; increment W by y, W will point at CFA?
0000E4r 2  20 rr rr         jsr addwx
0000E7r 2               
0000E7r 2               eval:
0000E7r 2               ; executing ? if status = 0
0000E7r 2  A5 E0            lda STATUS
0000E9r 2  F0 1F            beq execute
0000EBr 2               ;
0000EBr 2               ; falls thru on compile, but...
0000EBr 2               ; immediate ? if status+1 < 0 (bit seven set)
0000EBr 2  A5 E1            lda STATUS + 1
0000EDr 2  30 1B            bmi immediate
0000EFr 2               
0000EFr 2               compile:          ; otherwise compile
0000EFr 2               .ifdef DEBUG
0000EFr 2  A5 D6            lda DFLAG          ; DEBUG, print C if here
0000F1r 2  D0 0F            bne CMPSKIP
0000F3r 2  A9 0D 20 rr      WCRLF_np
0000F7r 2  rr A9 0A 20  
0000FBr 2  rr rr        
0000FDr 2  A9 43            lda #'C'
0000FFr 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
000102r 2               CMPSKIP:
000102r 2               .endif
000102r 2  20 rr rr         jsr wcomma          ; copy W into NEXTHEAP ('here'), increment NEXTHEAP
000105r 2  B0 03            bcs immediate
000107r 2  4C rr rr         jmp resolve         ; if not 'immediate' go on to next token
00010Ar 2               ;
00010Ar 2               immediate:
00010Ar 2               execute:
00010Ar 2               
00010Ar 2               .ifdef DEBUG
00010Ar 2  A5 D6            lda DFLAG         ; DEBUG, print E if here
00010Cr 2  D0 0F            bne EXESKIP
00010Er 2  A9 0D 20 rr      WCRLF_np
000112r 2  rr A9 0A 20  
000116r 2  rr rr        
000118r 2  A9 45            lda #'E'
00011Ar 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
00011Dr 2               EXESKIP:
00011Dr 2               .endif
00011Dr 2  A9 rr            lda #>resolvept     ; set up INSTPTR to run,
00011Fr 2  85 ED            sta INSTPTR + 1     ; or return to interpreter.
000121r 2  A9 rr            lda #<resolvept
000123r 2  85 EC            sta INSTPTR
000125r 2  4C rr rr         jmp pick             ; almost done, 'next' and either ENTER or EXEC
000128r 2               
000128r 2               ;-----------------------START PROCESSING INPUT-----------------------
000128r 2               try:
000128r 2  B1 D2            lda (TIB), y                   ; index is in y
00012Ar 2  F0 04            beq getline    ; if \0  - get a line if pointing at 0
00012Cr 2  C8               iny
00012Dr 2  49 20            eor #$20    ; return 0 in  A if a space
00012Fr 2  60               rts
000130r 2               
000130r 2               ;--------------------GET AN INPUT LINE ENDING WITH CR/LF ------------
000130r 2               getline:   ; drop rts of try, fall through to 'token'
000130r 2  68               pla
000131r 2  68               pla
000132r 2               ;
000132r 2               ;   DO AUTOLOAD HERE
000132r 2               ;      load a space, then copy next line to buffer
000132r 2               ;      calc y (length + 1) jump to GETLNEND
000132r 2               ;
000132r 2  A5 DF            lda ALFLAG
000134r 2  F0 06            beq GLNORMAL
000136r 2  20 rr rr         jsr ALOADTIB
000139r 2  4C rr rr         jmp GETLNSKIPCRLF
00013Cr 2               
00013Cr 2               GLNORMAL:
00013Cr 2  5A A0 00 B9      WSEQ_raw HYPROMPT    ; print prompt
000140r 2  rr rr F0 06  
000144r 2  C8 20 rr rr  
00014Br 2               ;
00014Br 2  A0 00            ldy #0   ; leave the first
00014Dr 2               GETLOOP:
00014Dr 2  91 D2            sta (TIB), y  ; dummy store on first pass, overwritten
00014Fr 2  C8               iny
000150r 2  C4 D4            cpy TIBEND
000152r 2  F0 3F            beq GETLNEND
000154r 2  C0 FF            cpy #$FF
000156r 2  D0 02            bne GETREADLOOP
000158r 2  A0 01            ldy #1
00015Ar 2               GETREADLOOP:
00015Ar 2  20 rr rr         jsr READ_CHAR_NE
00015Dr 2  90 FB            bcc GETREADLOOP
00015Fr 2  C9 09            cmp #$09         ; handle tab
000161r 2  D0 1F            bne GETLNNOTAB
000163r 2  48 A9 0D 20      WCRLF
000167r 2  rr rr A9 0A  
00016Br 2  20 rr rr 68  
00016Fr 2  A9 20            lda #$20          ; CRLF and indent
000171r 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
000174r 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
000177r 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
00017Ar 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
00017Dr 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
000180r 2  80 CB            bra GETLOOP
000182r 2               GETLNNOTAB:
000182r 2  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
000185r 2  C9 0D            cmp #$0D
000187r 2  F0 0A            beq GETLNEND
000189r 2  C9 08            cmp #$08         ; handle backspace
00018Br 2  D0 C0            bne GETLOOP
00018Dr 2  88               dey
00018Er 2  88               dey
00018Fr 2  B1 D2            lda (TIB), y      ; make sure prev char not overwritten
000191r 2  80 BA            bra GETLOOP
000193r 2               GETLNEND:                ; clear all if y eq \0
000193r 2  48 A9 0D 20      WCRLF                 ; macro, preserves A
000197r 2  rr rr A9 0A  
00019Br 2  20 rr rr 68  
00019Fr 2               GETLNSKIPCRLF:          ; SKIP to here if don't want CRLF
00019Fr 2  A9 20            lda #$20
0001A1r 2  5A               phy
0001A2r 2  A0 00            ldy #0
0001A4r 2  91 D2            sta (TIB), y       ; start with space
0001A6r 2  7A               ply
0001A7r 2  91 D2            sta (TIB), y        ; ends with space
0001A9r 2  A9 00            lda #0            ; mark eol with 0
0001ABr 2  C8               iny
0001ACr 2  91 D2            sta (TIB), y
0001AEr 2  88               dey
0001AFr 2               ; start it
0001AFr 2  85 E2            sta CURBUF
0001B1r 2               
0001B1r 2               ;------------------AWKWARD Brazilian english comment here...-----------
0001B1r 2               ; in place every token,
0001B1r 2               ; the counter is placed at last space before word
0001B1r 2               ; no rewinds
0001B1r 2               ;
0001B1r 2               ;  (i.e 'the length byte goes immediately before name of word')
0001B1r 2               ;
0001B1r 2               ;    to access the buffer after a line is entered, to something like...
0001B1r 2               ;
0001B1r 2               ;      jsr token
0001B1r 2               ;      ldy #0
0001B1r 2               ;      lda (NXTTOK), y       ; length will be in A
0001B1r 2               ;      .... do some shit ...
0001B1r 2               ;loop:
0001B1r 2               ;      iny
0001B1r 2               ;      lda (NXTTOK), y       ; first char of token
0001B1r 2               ;      cmp #$20
0001B1r 2               ;      beq done             ; if is space, token done
0001B1r 2               ;      ... do some shit ...
0001B1r 2               ;      bra loop             ; go get next char
0001B1r 2               ;      ... etc ....
0001B1r 2               ;done:
0001B1r 2               ;
0001B1r 2               token:
0001B1r 2  A4 E2            ldy CURBUF   ; last position on INBUF
0001B3r 2               
0001B3r 2               TOKENSKIP:   ; skip spaces
0001B3r 2  20 rr rr         jsr try
0001B6r 2  F0 FB            beq TOKENSKIP
0001B8r 2  88               dey   ; keep y == <start of input word> + 1
0001B9r 2  84 F8            sty NXTTOK
0001BBr 2               
0001BBr 2               TOKENSCAN:  ; scan spaces
0001BBr 2  20 rr rr         jsr try
0001BEr 2  D0 FB            bne TOKENSCAN
0001C0r 2  88               dey   ; keep y == <end of input word> + 1
0001C1r 2  84 E2            sty CURBUF
0001C3r 2               
0001C3r 2               TOKENDONE:  ; find size and store it;
0001C3r 2  98               tya
0001C4r 2  38               sec
0001C5r 2  E5 F8            sbc NXTTOK
0001C7r 2  A4 F8            ldy NXTTOK    ; keep it
0001C9r 2  88               dey
0001CAr 2  91 D2            sta (TIB), y  ; store size for counted string
0001CCr 2  84 F8            sty NXTTOK
0001CEr 2                   ;
0001CEr 2                   ;  During interpretive mode at least...do number and string capture here, before
0001CEr 2                   ;     looking at word list; will be pushed on stack.
0001CEr 2                   ;  This SHOULD have a general digit converter (any base up to 16); return C = 1 if no conversion
0001CEr 2                   ;   if SINGLE is defined, this will skip single digit numbers and use hardcoded ones.
0001CEr 2                   ;
0001CEr 2                   ;  Following check for #'s, check for quoted inline txt a la 'q^....^'
0001CEr 2                   ;
0001CEr 2               .ifdef numbers
0001CEr 2  A0 00            ldy #0
0001D0r 2  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
0001D2r 2  AA               tax                ; store size in X, pass to conversion
0001D3r 2  20 rr rr         jsr DIGCONVT
0001D6r 2  B0 02            bcs CHKFERTXT       ; if some error in conversion, skip and continue processing
0001D8r 2  80 0C            bra TOKCLR0
0001DAr 2               .endif  ; 'numbers'
0001DAr 2               CHKFERTXT:
0001DAr 2  20 rr rr         jsr TEXTGET         ; returns length +4 in X
0001DDr 2  B0 18            bcs TOKENEND
0001DFr 2  A0 00            ldy #0
0001E1r 2  A9 20            lda #$20
0001E3r 2  4C rr rr         jmp TOKCLR
0001E6r 2               TOKCLR0:
0001E6r 2  A0 00            ldy #0
0001E8r 2  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y     ; load length again
0001EAr 2  AA               tax
0001EBr 2  E8               inx
0001ECr 2  A9 20            lda #$20         ; copy spaces over entire converted string
0001EEr 2               TOKCLR:
0001EEr 2  91 F8            sta (NXTTOK),y
0001F0r 2  C8               iny
0001F1r 2  CA               dex
0001F2r 2  D0 FA            bne TOKCLR
0001F4r 2               TOKNEXT:
0001F4r 2  4C rr rr         jmp token               ; and use 'token' to rebuild input buffer w/o converted #
0001F7r 2               
0001F7r 2               
0001F7r 2               TOKENEND:
0001F7r 2  18               clc     ; clean - setup token
0001F8r 2  60               rts
0001F9r 2               ;
0001F9r 2               ;--------------------UTILITIES----------------------------------------
0001F9r 2               ;    A whole bunch of helper functions;
0001F9r 2               ;  wcomma / comma increment WORKREG (or another reg) and NEXTHEAP ('here')
0001F9r 2               ;  while copying into NEXTHEAP, uses incwx.
0001F9r 2               ;
0001F9r 2               ;  copyinto / copyfrom copy AND increment/decrement
0001F9r 2               ;  spush/rpush - pushes something onto stacks indexed on ZP by Y (increment using addwx/incwx)
0001F9r 2               ;  spull/rpull - pulls from stacks, copies into ZP indexed by Y (decrement builtin)
0001F9r 2               ;
0001F9r 2               ;  addwx/incwx/decwx do some of the inc/dec-rementing duties
0001F9r 2               ;
0001F9r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0001F9r 2               ;
0001F9r 2               ;   COMMA allocates memory at top of heap for 'other stuff'
0001F9r 2               ;   and makes sure heap and working reg pointers are updated.
0001F9r 2               ;
0001F9r 2               ; heap linked list (moves forward)
0001F9r 2               ;
0001F9r 2               wcomma:
0001F9r 2  A0 EE            ldy #WORKREG                  ; copy addr at WORKREG, change addr fld NEXTHEAP points to
0001FBr 2               comma:
0001FBr 2  A2 E6            ldx #NEXTHEAP                 ; Y has source of address, change addr fld NEXTHEAP points to
0001FDr 2                   ; FALL THROUGH - copyinto, then rts after second incwx
0001FDr 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0001FDr 2               ; from a page zero address indexed by Y
0001FDr 2               ; into a page zero indirect address indexed by X
0001FDr 2               ;
0001FDr 2               copyinto:
0001FDr 2  B9 00 00         lda 0, y
000200r 2  81 00            sta (0, x)
000202r 2  20 rr rr         jsr incwx
000205r 2  B9 01 00         lda 1, y
000208r 2  81 00            sta (0, x)
00020Ar 2  4C rr rr         jmp incwx                        ; incwx ends with rts!
00020Dr 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00020Dr 2               ;
00020Dr 2               ; generics - PUSH, PULL, incwx/addwx, copyfrom
00020Dr 2               ;
00020Dr 2               ;------------------------PUSH a cell--------------------------------
00020Dr 2               spush_2:
00020Dr 2  A0 F4            ldy #TEMP3       ; push TEMP3 on top
00020Fr 2  4C rr rr         jmp spush
000212r 2               spush_1:
000212r 2  A0 F2            ldy #TEMP2       ; push TEMP2 on top
000214r 2  4C rr rr         jmp spush
000217r 2               spush_0:             ; push TEMP1 to stack, probably top of stack
000217r 2  A0 F0            ldy #TEMP1
000219r 2                    ; FALL THROUGH
000219r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000219r 2               ; PUSH a cell
000219r 2               ; from a page zero address indexed by Y
000219r 2               ; into a page zero indirect address indexed by X
000219r 2               spush:
000219r 2  A2 E8            ldx #DSPTR
00021Br 2  A5 E8            lda DSPTR
00021Dr 2  C9 rr            cmp #<DS
00021Fr 2  F0 20            beq ptrerr_s            ; DS boundary check
000221r 2  4C rr rr         jmp push
000224r 2               rpush:
000224r 2  A2 EA            ldx #RTPTR
000226r 2  A5 EA            lda RTPTR
000228r 2  C9 rr            cmp #<RT                ; RT boundary check
00022Ar 2  F0 11            beq ptrerr_r
00022Cr 2               
00022Cr 2                   ; FALL THROUGH
00022Cr 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00022Cr 2               ; classic stack backwards
00022Cr 2               push:
00022Cr 2  20 rr rr         jsr decwx
00022Fr 2  B9 01 00         lda 1, y
000232r 2  81 00            sta (0, x)
000234r 2  20 rr rr         jsr decwx
000237r 2  B9 00 00         lda 0, y
00023Ar 2  81 00            sta (0, x)
00023Cr 2  60               rts
00023Dr 2               ;
00023Dr 2               ;                      pointer error (DS or RT)
00023Dr 2               ptrerr_r:                      ; pop jsr off stack, throw error
00023Dr 2  A9 01            lda #ERR_RPTR
00023Fr 2  80 02            bra ptrerr_cont
000241r 2               ptrerr_s:
000241r 2  A9 02            lda #ERR_SPTR
000243r 2               ptrerr_cont:
000243r 2  85 D7            sta ERRFLAG
000245r 2  68               pla
000246r 2  68               pla
000247r 2  4C rr rr         jmp errrtn
00024Ar 2               ;
00024Ar 2               ;---------------- PULL a cell, with convenience for TEMP 1/2/3 -----
00024Ar 2               ;
00024Ar 2               spull_2:
00024Ar 2  A0 F4            ldy #TEMP3              ; pull TEMP3 from top
00024Cr 2  4C rr rr         jmp spull
00024Fr 2               
00024Fr 2               spull_1:                    ; pull TEMP2 from top
00024Fr 2  A0 F2            ldy #TEMP2
000251r 2  4C rr rr         jmp spull
000254r 2               
000254r 2               spull_0:
000254r 2  A0 F0            ldy #TEMP1             ; pull TEMP1 from top of DS
000256r 2               ;
000256r 2               ;  FALL THROUGH
000256r 2               ;
000256r 2               ; PULL a cell
000256r 2               ; from a page zero indirect address indexed by X
000256r 2               ; into a page zero address indexed by y
000256r 2               ;
000256r 2               spull:
000256r 2  A2 E8            ldx #DSPTR
000258r 2  A5 E8            lda DSPTR         ; pointer bounds checking
00025Ar 2  C9 7E            cmp #DSEND        ; ditto
00025Cr 2  F0 E3            beq ptrerr_s      ; ditto
00025Er 2  4C rr rr         jmp pull          ; pull includes rts from incwx so....
000261r 2               rpull:                ;
000261r 2  A2 EA            ldx #RTPTR
000263r 2  A5 EA            lda RTPTR        ; pointer bounds checking
000265r 2  C9 FE            cmp #RTEND        ; ditto
000267r 2  F0 D4            beq ptrerr_r        ; ditto
000269r 2               ;
000269r 2               ;  FALL THROUGH
000269r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000269r 2               ;
000269r 2               ; from a page zero indirect address indexed by X
000269r 2               ; into a page zero address indexed by y
000269r 2               pull:
000269r 2               copyfrom:
000269r 2  A1 00            lda (0, x)
00026Br 2  99 00 00         sta 0, y
00026Er 2  20 rr rr         jsr incwx      ; NOTE:  not a jmp, returns here.
000271r 2  A1 00            lda (0, x)
000273r 2  99 01 00         sta 1, y
000276r 2                ;
000276r 2                ;  FALL THROUGH
000276r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000276r 2               ; increment a word in page zero, identified by X and offset to it from $00.
000276r 2               ;
000276r 2               ;   Usage: ldx #<pointer name> and then jsr incwx/addwx
000276r 2               ;
000276r 2               ;   THESE functions are SOLELY intended to increment/decrement
000276r 2               ;   zeropage pointers.  Offsets indexed by
000276r 2               ;   X are INTO the zeropage space, not relative to a specific
000276r 2               ;   pointer location; X IDENTIFIES the 16-bit pointer in
000276r 2               ;   question, period.
000276r 2               ;
000276r 2               incwx:
000276r 2  A9 01            lda #01
000278r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000278r 2               ; add a byte in A to a word in page zero. X identifies the pointer
000278r 2               ; being incremented, and is not itself dec'd or inc'd.
000278r 2               ;
000278r 2               addwx:
000278r 2  18               clc
000279r 2  75 00            adc 0, x
00027Br 2  95 00            sta 0, x
00027Dr 2  90 03            bcc addwx_end
00027Fr 2  F6 01            inc 1, x
000281r 2  18               clc      ; keep carry clean.
000282r 2                            ; our convention is that functions SET the carry for positive results,
000282r 2                            ; clear carry for negative or neutral ones.
000282r 2               addwx_end:
000282r 2  60               rts
000283r 2               
000283r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000283r 2               ;          decwx, heap moves
000283r 2               ; decrement a word in page zero, identified by X and offset to it from $00.
000283r 2               ;
000283r 2               ;   Usage: ldx #<pointer name> and then jsr decwx
000283r 2               ;
000283r 2               ;   THESE functions are SOLELY intended to increment/decrement
000283r 2               ;   zeropage pointers.  Offsets indexed by
000283r 2               ;   X are INTO the zeropage space, not relative to a specific
000283r 2               ;   pointer location; X IDENTIFIES the 16-bit pointer in
000283r 2               ;   question, period.
000283r 2               ;
000283r 2               decwx:
000283r 2  B5 00            lda 0, x
000285r 2  D0 02            bne decwx_end
000287r 2  D6 01            dec 1, x
000289r 2               decwx_end:
000289r 2  D6 00            dec 0, x
00028Br 2  60               rts
00028Cr 2               ;
00028Cr 2               ;
00028Cr 2               ;
00028Cr 2               ENGINEEND:
00028Cr 2               ;                           END OF CORE ENGINE
00028Cr 2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
00028Cr 2               ;
00028Cr 2               ;                        DICTIONARY and ADDITIONS
00028Cr 2               ;
00028Cr 2               ;    upper.s -- error messaging, CLEAR on start, debug, utilities
00028Cr 2               ;------------------------------------------------------------------------
00028Cr 2               ;
00028Cr 2               upper_ram:
00028Cr 2                  .include "upper.s"
00028Cr 3               ;
00028Cr 3               ;   upper.s  - utility functions for HyForth - eventually ROM resident
00028Cr 3               ;
00028Cr 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00028Cr 3               ;  error messaging
00028Cr 3               ;
00028Cr 3               wrterror:
00028Cr 3  A9 rr            lda #>err_jumptable
00028Er 3  85 D9            sta ERRPTR+1
000290r 3  A5 D7            lda ERRFLAG
000292r 3  F0 36            beq ERREND
000294r 3  0A               asl a
000295r 3  18               clc
000296r 3  69 rr            adc #<err_jumptable
000298r 3  85 D8            sta ERRPTR
00029Ar 3  90 02            bcc ERRSKIP
00029Cr 3  E6 D9            inc ERRPTR+1
00029Er 3               ERRSKIP:
00029Er 3  A9 0D 20 rr      WERR ERRPTR
0002A2r 3  rr A9 0A 20  
0002A6r 3  rr rr A0 00  
0002CAr 3               ERREND:
0002CAr 3  A9 00            lda #0
0002CCr 3  85 D7            sta ERRFLAG
0002CEr 3  85 D8            sta ERRPTR
0002D0r 3  85 D9            sta ERRPTR+1
0002D2r 3  60               rts
0002D3r 3               ;
0002D3r 3               ;
0002D3r 3               err_jumptable:
0002D3r 3  xx xx            .res 2
0002D5r 3  rr rr            ERR_entry RPTR_ERR              ; RT stack full/empty  - error $01
0002D7r 3  rr rr            ERR_entry SPTR_ERR              ; DS stack full/empty  - error $02
0002D9r 3  rr rr            ERR_entry DIV_ERR               ; divide by zero - error $03
0002DBr 3  rr rr            ERR_entry OOM_ERR               ; out of memory  - error $04
0002DDr 3  rr rr            ERR_entry UKW_ERR               ; no existing word - error $05
0002DFr 3  rr rr            ERR_entry SEC_ERR               ; writing to dangerous RAM areas - error $06
0002E1r 3  rr rr            ERR_entry SYS_ERR               ; error on return from SYSCALL - error $07
0002E3r 3               LASTERR = 7
0002E3r 3               ;
0002E3r 3               ;  error messages
0002E3r 3               RPTR_ERR:
0002E3r 3  20 21 52 54      .byte " !RT PTR ERROR!"
0002E7r 3  20 50 54 52  
0002EBr 3  20 45 52 52  
0002F2r 3  00               .byte 0
0002F3r 3               SPTR_ERR:
0002F3r 3  20 21 44 53      .byte " !DS PTR ERROR!"
0002F7r 3  20 50 54 52  
0002FBr 3  20 45 52 52  
000302r 3  00               .byte 0
000303r 3               DIV_ERR:

00033Dr 3               ;-------------------------------------------------------------
00033Dr 3               ;
00033Dr 3               ;           CODE called by engine AND heap
00033Dr 3               ;
00033Dr 3               ;------- used by 'exit'
00033Dr 3               ;------------------------NOTE:  this is ALL of 'exit' but the screaming.
00033Dr 3               ;----------------- For modularity purposes this is the only sensible place for it.
00033Dr 3               ;
00033Dr 3               EXIT:
00033Dr 3               unnest:                             ; EXIT - done with previous word, on to next whether compiled or primitive
00033Dr 3               
00033Dr 3  A0 EC            ldy #INSTPTR
00033Fr 3  20 rr rr         jsr rpull                       ; IP = [RTPTR], RTPTR += 2
000342r 3               
000342r 3               next:                               ;    go on to next word; IP is pointing at next entry in data field of word
000342r 3               ; WORKREG = (INSTPTR) ; INSTPTR += 2
000342r 3  A2 EC            ldx #INSTPTR
000344r 3  A0 EE            ldy #WORKREG
000346r 3  20 rr rr         jsr copyfrom                    ; W = [IP], IP += 2  ( and either ENTER or EXECUTE )
000349r 3               
000349r 3               pick:                               ;        'THE SWITCHER' - COMPILED OR PRIMITIVE?
000349r 3                                                   ;
000349r 3               .ifdef DEBUG                        ;  DEBUG
000349r 3  A5 D6            lda DFLAG
00034Br 3  D0 2F            bne PRINTWSKIP                  ; print W and [W] etc if DEBUG
00034Dr 3  5A A0 00 B9      WSEQ_raw WDISP
000351r 3  rr rr F0 06  
000355r 3  C8 20 rr rr  
00035Cr 3  A5 EF            lda WORKREG+1
00035Er 3  20 rr rr         PRINT_BYTE
000361r 3  A5 EE            lda WORKREG
000363r 3  20 rr rr         PRINT_BYTE
000366r 3  5A A0 00 B9      WSEQ_raw WATDISP
00036Ar 3  rr rr F0 06  
00036Er 3  C8 20 rr rr  
000375r 3  A0 00            ldy #0
000377r 3  B1 EE            lda (WORKREG),y
000379r 3  20 rr rr         PRINT_BYTE
00037Cr 3               PRINTWSKIP:
00037Cr 3               .endif
00037Cr 3               ; .. use old-fashioned way AND new tricks
00037Cr 3               ;  ( otherwise known as THE SWITCH )
00037Cr 3               ;
00037Cr 3  A5 EF            lda WORKREG + 1                 ; compare pages (MSBs)
00037Er 3  C9 rr            cmp #>ends + 1                  ;  !! Compiled must be higher in memory than hardcoded !! (see below)
000380r 3  30 16            bmi jump                        ; jump over nest if native code
000382r 3  A0 01            ldy #1                          ; ldy #0 if check extra byte ($89 or $24 for various 'bit' variants)
000384r 3  B1 EE            lda (WORKREG),y
000386r 3  F0 10            beq jump                        ; yep, jump to native execution
000388r 3               
000388r 3               nest:                               ; ENTER in classic Forth lingo   ( COMPILED )
000388r 3  A0 EC            ldy #INSTPTR
00038Ar 3  20 rr rr         jsr rpush                       ; RTPTR -=2, [RTPTR] = [IP]
00038Dr 3  A5 EE            lda WORKREG                     ; W = IP
00038Fr 3  85 EC            sta INSTPTR
000391r 3  A5 EF            lda WORKREG + 1
000393r 3  85 ED            sta INSTPTR + 1
000395r 3  4C rr rr         jmp next                        ; next
000398r 3               
000398r 3               jump:                               ; EXECUTE                      ( PRIMITIVE )
000398r 3  6C EE 00         jmp (WORKREG)                   ; start running code at next word
00039Br 3                                                   ;  JUMP [W]
00039Br 3               ;
00039Br 3               ; other stuff we want in ROM
00039Br 3               ;

0003E1r 3               MALLOC:
0003E1r 3                   ;  TEMP1 and TEMP2 should have bytes / record type if 'jsr MALLOC'
0003E1r 3                   ;  uses TEMP3, y, x, a
0003E1r 3  A5 D0            lda MEMLAST

0003E6r 3  85 D0            sta MEMLAST
0003E8r 3  B0 02            bcs MALSK00
0003EAr 3  C6 D1            dec MEMLAST+1

0003F3r 3  E5 F1            sbc TEMP1+1
0003F5r 3  85 D1            sta MEMLAST+1
0003F7r 3                                       ;MEMLAST updated to start of new record

0003F9r 3  85 F4            sta TEMP3
0003FBr 3  A5 D1            lda MEMLAST+1
0003FDr 3  85 F5            sta TEMP3+1         ; use TEMP3 to walk through clearing of memory
0003FFr 3  A0 00            ldy #0
000401r 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2            ; write type first
000403r 3  91 F4            sta (TEMP3),y
000405r 3  C8               iny

000416r 3               MALLOOP:

000468r 3               ;
000468r 3               ;-------------------------------------------------------------
000468r 3               ;                MATH routines
000468r 3               ;         with MULT16 / DIV16, signs handled by calling word.
000468r 3               ;         we just do the math here.
000468r 3               ;
000468r 3               ;    transition to Z_MATH1,2,3,4 to replace use of TEMP1,2,3,4
000468r 3               ;
000468r 3               ;
000468r 3               MULT16:                             ; 16 x 16 multiply; Z_MATH1 and Z_MATH2 are #'s, Z_MATH1 will be result
000468r 3  64 B6            stz Z_MATH3                       ; with Z_MATH3 as high bytes
00046Ar 3  64 B7            stz Z_MATH3+1
00046Cr 3  A2 11            ldx #17
00046Er 3  18               clc
00046Fr 3               MULTLOOP:
00046Fr 3  66 B7            ror Z_MATH3+1                     ; RIGHT.  if you need to go backwards, go backwards stupid fuck.
000471r 3  66 B6            ror Z_MATH3
000473r 3  66 B3            ror Z_MATH1+1
000475r 3  66 B2            ror Z_MATH1
000477r 3  90 0D            bcc MULTDECCNT
000479r 3  18               clc
00047Ar 3  A5 B4            lda Z_MATH2
00047Cr 3  65 B6            adc Z_MATH3
00047Er 3  85 B6            sta Z_MATH3
000480r 3  A5 B5            lda Z_MATH2+1
000482r 3  65 B7            adc Z_MATH3+1
000484r 3  85 B7            sta Z_MATH3+1
000486r 3               MULTDECCNT:
000486r 3  CA               dex
000487r 3  D0 E6            bne MULTLOOP
000489r 3  60               rts
00048Ar 3               
00048Ar 3               ;
00048Ar 3               ;
00048Ar 3               ;           16 / 16 divide; Z_MATH1 and Z_MATH2 #'s - Z_MATH3 is 'overflow'
00048Ar 3               ;           Z_MATH2 divisor, Z_MATH1 dividend, Z_MATH1 + 3 = result + remainder
00048Ar 3               DIV16:
00048Ar 3  64 B6            stz Z_MATH3
00048Cr 3  64 B7            stz Z_MATH3+1
00048Er 3  A2 10            ldx #16
000490r 3               UDIVLP:
000490r 3  26 B2            rol Z_MATH1
000492r 3  26 B3            rol Z_MATH1+1
000494r 3  26 B6            rol Z_MATH3
000496r 3  26 B7            rol Z_MATH3+1
000498r 3               UDIVCHK:
000498r 3  38               sec
000499r 3  A5 B6            lda Z_MATH3
00049Br 3  E5 B4            sbc Z_MATH2
00049Dr 3  A8               tay
00049Er 3  A5 B7            lda Z_MATH3+1
0004A0r 3  E5 B5            sbc Z_MATH2+1
0004A2r 3  90 04            bcc UDIVCNT
0004A4r 3  84 B6            sty Z_MATH3
0004A6r 3  85 B7            sta Z_MATH3+1
0004A8r 3               UDIVCNT:
0004A8r 3  CA               dex
0004A9r 3  D0 E5            bne UDIVLP
0004ABr 3  26 B2            rol Z_MATH1
0004ADr 3  26 B3            rol Z_MATH1+1
0004AFr 3  60               rts
0004B0r 3               ;
0004B0r 3               ;        32 / 16 divide; Z_MATH2-Z_MATH1 / Z_MATH3 #'s - Z_MATH4 is 'overflow'
0004B0r 3               ;        -> Z_MATH2-Z_MATH1 + Z_MATH4 = result + remainder
0004B0r 3               DIV32:
0004B0r 3  64 B8            stz Z_MATH4
0004B2r 3  64 B9            stz Z_MATH4+1
0004B4r 3  A2 10            ldx #16
0004B6r 3               UDIV32LP:
0004B6r 3  26 B2            rol Z_MATH1
0004B8r 3  26 B3            rol Z_MATH1+1
0004BAr 3  26 B4            rol Z_MATH2
0004BCr 3  26 B5            rol Z_MATH2+1
0004BEr 3  26 B8            rol Z_MATH4
0004C0r 3  26 B9            rol Z_MATH4+1
0004C2r 3               UDIV32CHK:
0004C2r 3  38               sec
0004C3r 3  A5 B8            lda Z_MATH4
0004C5r 3  E5 B6            sbc Z_MATH3
0004C7r 3  A8               tay
0004C8r 3  A5 B9            lda Z_MATH4+1
0004CAr 3  E5 B7            sbc Z_MATH3+1
0004CCr 3  90 04            bcc UDIV32CNT
0004CEr 3  84 B8            sty Z_MATH4
0004D0r 3  85 B9            sta Z_MATH4+1
0004D2r 3               UDIV32CNT:
0004D2r 3  CA               dex
0004D3r 3  D0 E1            bne UDIV32LP
0004D5r 3  26 B2            rol Z_MATH1
0004D7r 3  26 B3            rol Z_MATH1+1
0004D9r 3  26 B4            rol Z_MATH2
0004DBr 3  26 B5            rol Z_MATH2+1
0004DDr 3  60               rts
0004DEr 3               ;
0004DEr 3               ;     galois32o - LSFR psuedo-random # generator
0004DEr 3               ;
0004DEr 3               ;  -- boilerplate --
0004DEr 3               ; 6502 LFSR PRNG - 32-bit
0004DEr 3               ; Brad Smith, 2019
0004DEr 3               ; http://rainwarrior.ca
0004DEr 3               ;
0004DEr 3               ;
0004DEr 3               galois32o:
0004DEr 3                   ; rotate the middle bytes left
0004DEr 3  A4 DD            ldy RSEED+2                     ; will move to RSEED+3 at the end
0004E0r 3  A5 DC            lda RSEED+1
0004E2r 3  85 DD            sta RSEED+2
0004E4r 3                   ; compute RSEED+1 ($C5>>1 = %1100010)
0004E4r 3  A5 DE            lda RSEED+3                     ; original high byte
0004E6r 3  4A               lsr
0004E7r 3  85 DC            sta RSEED+1                     ; reverse: 100011
0004E9r 3  4A               lsr
0004EAr 3  4A               lsr
0004EBr 3  4A               lsr
0004ECr 3  4A               lsr
0004EDr 3  45 DC            eor RSEED+1
0004EFr 3  4A               lsr
0004F0r 3  45 DC            eor RSEED+1
0004F2r 3  45 DB            eor RSEED+0                     ; combine with original low byte
0004F4r 3  85 DC            sta RSEED+1
0004F6r 3                   ; compute RSEED+0 ($C5 = %11000101)
0004F6r 3  A5 DE            lda RSEED+3                     ; original high byte
0004F8r 3  0A               asl
0004F9r 3  45 DE            eor RSEED+3
0004FBr 3  0A               asl
0004FCr 3  0A               asl
0004FDr 3  0A               asl
0004FEr 3  0A               asl
0004FFr 3  45 DE            eor RSEED+3
000501r 3  0A               asl
000502r 3  0A               asl
000503r 3  45 DE            eor RSEED+3
000505r 3  84 DE            sty RSEED+3                     ; finish rotating byte 2 into 3
000507r 3  85 DB            sta RSEED+0
000509r 3  60               rts
00050Ar 3               ;-------------------------------------------------------------------
00050Ar 3               ;              get delimited text from INBUF, store in string
00050Ar 3               ;
00050Ar 3               ;
00050Ar 3               ;  uses TEMP1, TEMP2, TEMP3, TEMP5, TEMP6, X, Y
00050Ar 3               ;
00050Ar 3               TEXTGET:
00050Ar 3  64 F1            stz TEMP1+1                     ; will store length here
00050Cr 3  A9 04            lda #$04
00050Er 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2                       ; record type is 'sz'
000510r 3  64 F3            stz TEMP2+1
000512r 3  64 FD            stz TEMP6                       ; to save y for later copy
000514r 3  A0 01            ldy #1                          ; skip len of first token
000516r 3               TX2SKSPC:
000516r 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
000518r 3  C9 20            cmp #$20                        ; skip leading spaces
00051Ar 3  D0 03            bne TX2SK00
00051Cr 3  C8               iny
00051Dr 3  80 F7            bra TX2SKSPC
00051Fr 3               TX2SK00:
00051Fr 3  C9 71            cmp #'q'
000521r 3  D0 63            bne TX2NOGOOD
000523r 3  C8               iny
000524r 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
000526r 3  C9 5E            cmp #'^'
000528r 3  D0 5C            bne TX2NOGOOD
00052Ar 3  C8               iny
00052Br 3  84 FD            sty TEMP6                       ; temp6 stores pos of first char
00052Dr 3               TX2SCAN:
00052Dr 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y                  ; find delimiting '^'
00052Fr 3  C8               iny
000530r 3  C9 5E            cmp #'^'
000532r 3  F0 0A            beq TX2FOUND
000534r 3  98               tya
000535r 3  18               clc
000536r 3  65 F8            adc NXTTOK
000538r 3  C9 64            cmp #MAXSTR
00053Ar 3  B0 4A            bcs TX2NOGOOD
00053Cr 3  80 EF            bra TX2SCAN
00053Er 3               TX2FOUND:
00053Er 3  88               dey
00053Fr 3  84 FC            sty TEMP5                       ; temp5 pos+1 last letter
000541r 3  98               tya
000542r 3  38               sec
000543r 3  E5 FD            sbc TEMP6
000545r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1                       ; and this should be the length
000547r 3  E6 F0            inc TEMP1                       ; and add one for zero at end
000549r 3               
000549r 3  20 rr rr         jsr MALLOC                      ; TEMP1 now has address on mem stack
00054Cr 3               
00054Cr 3  A0 00            ldy #0
00054Er 3  B1 F0            lda (TEMP1),y
000550r 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2
000552r 3  C8               iny
000553r 3  B1 F0            lda (TEMP1),y
000555r 3  85 F3            sta TEMP2+1                     ; address in memory area
000557r 3               
000557r 3                   ; now for math.  NXTTOK ptr needs to be ref'd with same y
000557r 3                   ; as TEMP2, so we need to match them up...and we are hitting
000557r 3                   ; the record at +3, so....TEMP6 is start
000557r 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2
000559r 3  38               sec
00055Ar 3  E5 FD            sbc TEMP6
00055Cr 3  B0 02            bcs TX2SK01
00055Er 3  C6 F3            dec TEMP2+1
000560r 3               TX2SK01:

000567r 3               TX2SK02:
000567r 3  A4 FD            ldy TEMP6
000569r 3               TX2CPYLOOP:

00056Br 3  91 F2            sta (TEMP2),y
00056Dr 3  C8               iny
00056Er 3  C4 FC            cpy TEMP5
000570r 3  D0 F7            bne TX2CPYLOOP
000572r 3               TX2SK99:

00059Er 3               ;------------------------
00059Er 3               ;      CONVERT DIGITS, PUSH on DS
00059Er 3               
00059Er 3               DIGCONVT:    ;  Y is index into NXTPTR, X is length
00059Er 3  64 F0            stz TEMP1
0005A0r 3  64 F1            stz TEMP1+1
0005A2r 3  64 F2            stz TEMP2                       ; no necc for bin or hex, but
0005A4r 3  64 F3            stz TEMP2+1                     ; since is convenient....
0005A6r 3  64 FD            stz TEMP6
0005A8r 3  A0 01            ldy #1                          ; skip over length
0005AAr 3  A9 0A            lda #10
0005ACr 3  85 DA            sta DIGBASE
0005AEr 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
0005B0r 3  C9 2D            cmp #'-'
0005B2r 3  F0 0A            beq DIGCONV_MINUS
0005B4r 3  C9 24            cmp #'$'
0005B6r 3  F0 12            beq DIGHEX1
0005B8r 3  C9 25            cmp #'%'
0005BAr 3  F0 07            beq DIGBIN1
0005BCr 3  80 12            bra DIG_SNG
0005BEr 3               DIGCONV_MINUS:
0005BEr 3  E6 FD            inc TEMP6
0005C0r 3  4C rr rr         jmp DIG_XY
0005C3r 3               DIGBIN1:

0005C5r 3  85 DA            sta DIGBASE
0005C7r 3  4C rr rr         jmp DIG_XY
0005CAr 3               DIGHEX1:
0005CAr 3  A9 10            lda #16
0005CCr 3  85 DA            sta DIGBASE
0005CEr 3               DIG_XY:
0005CEr 3  C8               iny
0005CFr 3  CA               dex
0005D0r 3               DIG_SNG:
0005D0r 3                 .ifdef SINGLE
0005D0r 3  E0 01            cpx #1                          ; skip single digit, handle hard-wired or...
0005D2r 3  D0 03            bne DIGCONV_LOOP                ; only one digit left, we can handle that elsewhere
0005D4r 3  4C rr rr         jmp DIGCONV_ERR                 ; not really an error, just return and let 'find' to it
0005D7r 3                 .endif
0005D7r 3               DIGCONV_LOOP:
0005D7r 3  20 rr rr         jsr GETDIG
0005DAr 3  90 03            bcc DIGCONT0
0005DCr 3  4C rr rr         jmp DIGCONV_ERR
0005DFr 3               DIGCONT0:
0005DFr 3  48               pha                             ; else save it to add in a bit
0005E0r 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1                       ; do first shift
0005E2r 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1
0005E4r 3  A5 DA            lda DIGBASE                     ; check base that was set above
0005E6r 3  C9 0A            cmp #10
0005E8r 3  F0 06            beq DIGDEC                      ; it's DEC, go there
0005EAr 3  C9 02            cmp #2
0005ECr 3  F0 2F            beq DIGBIN                      ; it's BIN, go there
0005EEr 3  80 1D            bra DIGHEX                      ; Go to hex if others not true
0005F0r 3               DIGDEC:
0005F0r 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1                       ; 2nd shift; upper nybble doesn't end up right
0005F2r 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1
0005F4r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1                       ; load results of two shifts
0005F6r 3  18               clc
0005F7r 3  65 F2            adc TEMP2                       ; add in previous total from last loop
0005F9r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
0005FBr 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1                     ; and same with second digit, and the carry
0005FDr 3  65 F3            adc TEMP2+1
0005FFr 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
000601r 3               DIGDEC2:
000601r 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1                       ; final shift
000603r 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1
000605r 3  68               pla                             ; bring back read digit
000606r 3  C9 0A            cmp #10                         ; make sure it's not hex, mostly
000608r 3  90 18            bcc DIGCONT                     ; jump to continue
00060Ar 3  4C rr rr         jmp DIGCONV_ERR                 ; else throw error
00060Dr 3               DIGHEX:
00060Dr 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1
00060Fr 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1
000611r 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1
000613r 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1
000615r 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1
000617r 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1                     ; shifted three more times
000619r 3  68               pla
00061Ar 3  4C rr rr         jmp DIGCONT
00061Dr 3               DIGBIN:                             ; already did the single shift
00061Dr 3  68               pla
00061Er 3  C9 02            cmp #2
000620r 3  B0 3E            bcs DIGCONV_ERR                 ; fall through to the additon of new digit
000622r 3               DIGCONT:
000622r 3  18               clc                             ; add digit finally
000623r 3  65 F0            adc TEMP1
000625r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
000627r 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2                       ; copy to intermediate result in case another dec digit
000629r 3  90 02            bcc DIGCONT2
00062Br 3  E6 F1            inc TEMP1+1                     ; and inc 2nd byte if necc.
00062Dr 3               DIGCONT2:
00062Dr 3  A5 DA            lda DIGBASE
00062Fr 3  C9 0A            cmp #10
000631r 3  D0 08            bne DIGCONT3
000633r 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
000635r 3  85 F3            sta TEMP2+1                     ; save it for next round, regardless
000637r 3  C9 80            cmp #$80                        ; check to see if > $8000
000639r 3  B0 25            bcs DIGCONV_ERR
00063Br 3               DIGCONT3:
00063Br 3  C8               iny
00063Cr 3  CA               dex
00063Dr 3  D0 98            bne DIGCONV_LOOP
00063Fr 3  A5 DA            lda DIGBASE
000641r 3  C9 0A            cmp #10
000643r 3  D0 16            bne DIGCONT4
000645r 3               DECFINISH:
000645r 3  A5 FD            lda TEMP6                       ; check for minus
000647r 3  F0 12            beq DIGCONT4
000649r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
00064Br 3  49 FF            eor #$FF
00064Dr 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
00064Fr 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
000651r 3  49 FF            eor #$FF
000653r 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
000655r 3  E6 F0            inc TEMP1
000657r 3  D0 02            bne DIGCONT4
000659r 3  E6 F1            inc TEMP1+1
00065Br 3               DIGCONT4:
00065Br 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_0                     ; push TEMP1 on to stack
00065Er 3  18               clc
00065Fr 3  60               rts
000660r 3               DIGCONV_ERR:
000660r 3  38               sec
000661r 3  60               rts
000662r 3               
000662r 3               ;--------------------------
000662r 3               GETDIG:                             ; y is index to next char in NXTTOK
000662r 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
000664r 3  38               sec
000665r 3  E9 30            sbc #'0'
000667r 3  90 10            bcc GETDIG_ERR
000669r 3  C9 0A            cmp #10
00066Br 3  90 0A            bcc GETDIG_RTN
00066Dr 3  E9 07            sbc #7
00066Fr 3  C9 0A            cmp #10
000671r 3  90 06            bcc GETDIG_ERR
000673r 3  C9 10            cmp #16
000675r 3  B0 02            bcs GETDIG_ERR
000677r 3               GETDIG_RTN:                         ; pass carry clear for good digit
000677r 3  18               clc
000678r 3  60               rts
000679r 3               GETDIG_ERR:                         ; pass carry set for no digit
000679r 3  38               sec
00067Ar 3  60               rts
00067Br 3               ;  end of new number conv
00067Br 3               ;
00067Br 3  27 10        H2NUM: .byte $27,$10
00067Dr 3  03 E8         .byte $03,$E8
00067Fr 3  00 64         .byte $00,$64
000681r 3  00 0A         .byte $00,$0A
000683r 3               HEX2DEC:                            ; low/high in A,Y - use X, TEMP1, Z_MATH2-4
000683r 3  84 B5            sty Z_MATH2+1
000685r 3  85 B4            sta Z_MATH2
000687r 3  A2 00            ldx #0
000689r 3               H2DDIV10:
000689r 3  BD rr rr         lda H2NUM,x
00068Cr 3  85 B7            sta Z_MATH3+1
00068Er 3  E8               inx
00068Fr 3  BD rr rr         lda H2NUM,x
000692r 3  85 B6            sta Z_MATH3
000694r 3  E8               inx
000695r 3  64 B8            stz Z_MATH4
000697r 3               H2DLOOP:
000697r 3  A5 B5            lda Z_MATH2+1
000699r 3  C5 B7            cmp Z_MATH3+1
00069Br 3  90 19            bcc  H2DSK1
00069Dr 3  D0 06            bne  H2DSK0
00069Fr 3  A5 B4            lda Z_MATH2
0006A1r 3  C5 B6            cmp Z_MATH3
0006A3r 3  90 11            bcc  H2DSK1
0006A5r 3               H2DSK0:

0006A7r 3  38               sec
0006A8r 3  E5 B6            sbc Z_MATH3
0006AAr 3  85 B4            sta Z_MATH2
0006ACr 3  A5 B5            lda Z_MATH2+1
0006AEr 3  E5 B7            sbc Z_MATH3+1
0006B0r 3  85 B5            sta Z_MATH2+1
0006B2r 3  E6 B8            inc Z_MATH4
0006B4r 3  80 E1            bra H2DLOOP
0006B6r 3               H2DSK1:

0006B8r 3  18               clc

0006BFr 3  DA               phx

0006CBr 3               H2DFIN:

0006D0r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1

0006D4r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_0
0006D7r 3  60               rts
0006D8r 3               ;
0006D8r 3               ;
0006D8r 3               .endif    ; numbers
0006D8r 3               ;--------------------------------- CLEAR ------------------------------

0006DCr 3               zpclear:
