0008FDr 3               COPYLOOP:
0008FDr 3  B1 rr            lda (ZP_TEMP_VEC),y
0008FFr 3  91 rr            sta (ZP_TEMP_VEC3),y
000901r 3  A5 rr            lda ZP_TEMP_VEC
000903r 3  C5 rr            cmp ZP_TEMP_VEC2
000905r 3  D0 06            bne SKIP1
000907r 3  A5 rr            lda ZP_TEMP_VEC+1
000909r 3  C5 rr            cmp ZP_TEMP_VEC2+1
00090Br 3  F0 17            beq CPRTS
00090Dr 3               SKIP1:
00090Dr 3  A5 FC            lda supprint
00090Fr 3  D0 05            bne SKIP01
000911r 3  A9 2E            lda #'.'
000913r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
000916r 3               SKIP01:
000916r 3  E6 rr            inc ZP_TEMP_VEC
000918r 3  D0 02            bne SKIP2
00091Ar 3  E6 rr            inc ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
00091Cr 3               SKIP2:
00091Cr 3  E6 rr            inc ZP_TEMP_VEC3
00091Er 3  D0 02            bne SKIP3
000920r 3  E6 rr            inc ZP_TEMP_VEC3+1
000922r 3               SKIP3:
000922r 3  80 D9            bra COPYLOOP
000924r 3               CPRTS:
000924r 3  60               rts
000925r 3               ;
000925r 3               ;  ANSI CLS, cursor reposition, and color change
000925r 3               ;
000925r 3               ANSIHDR:
000925r 3  A9 1B            lda #27
000927r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
00092Ar 3  A9 5B            lda #91
00092Cr 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
00092Fr 3  60               rts
000930r 3               
000930r 3               ANSIONEDIG:   ;two ASCII digits in Z_ANSI1
000930r 3  A5 BB            lda Z_ANSI1 + 1
000932r 3  C9 30            cmp #$30
000934r 3  F0 03            beq ansi_scr_00
000936r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
000939r 3               ansi_scr_00:
000939r 3  A5 BA            lda Z_ANSI1
00093Br 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
00093Er 3  60               rts
00093Fr 3               
00093Fr 3               ANSICLS:
00093Fr 3  20 rr rr         jsr ANSIHDR
000942r 3  A9 32            lda #'2'
000944r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
000947r 3  A9 4A            lda #'J'
000949r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
00094Cr 3  60               rts
00094Dr 3               
00094Dr 3               ANSISCR:      ; assumes Z_ANSI1 AND Z_ANSI2 have digits
00094Dr 3                             ; one or two digit ASCII #'s already
00094Dr 3  20 rr rr         jsr ANSIHDR
000950r 3  20 rr rr         jsr ANSIONEDIG
000953r 3  A9 3B            lda #';'
000955r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
000958r 3  A5 BC            lda Z_ANSI2
00095Ar 3  85 BA            sta Z_ANSI1
00095Cr 3  A5 BD            lda Z_ANSI2+1
00095Er 3  85 BB            sta Z_ANSI1+1
000960r 3  20 rr rr         jsr ANSIONEDIG
000963r 3  A9 66            lda #'f'
000965r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
000968r 3  60               rts
000969r 3               
000969r 3               ANSICOL:                 ; assumes Z_ANSI1 has digits
000969r 3  20 rr rr         jsr ANSIHDR
00096Cr 3  20 rr rr         jsr ANSIONEDIG
00096Fr 3  A9 6D            lda #'m'
000971r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
000974r 3  60               rts
000975r 3               ;
000975r 3               ; ---------------- end of upper.s
000975r 3               ;
000975r 3               ;   hyf_edit.s is loaded in hyforth.s directly after this
000975r 3               ;
000975r 3               
000975r 2               UPPER_END:
000975r 2               ;------------------------------------------------------------------------
000975r 2               ;
000975r 2               ;   hyf_edit.s -- the HyFedit screen editor stuff
000975r 2               ;
000975r 2                  .include "hyf_edit.s"
000975r 3               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
000975r 3               ;
000975r 3               ;  Patrick Struthers - October/November 2026
000975r 3               ;
000975r 3               ; *** hyf_edit.s
000975r 3               ;
000975r 3               ; HyFedit - screen editor for Hydra-16 / HyForth
000975r 3               ;
000975r 3               ; NOTES:  for now this is included at the tail of 'upper.s' in
000975r 3               ;   the main hyforth build, but has a seperate ROM space
000975r 3               ;   at $F500 per the os_rom.cfg file.  May just add it to
000975r 3               ;   tail of core Forth OS ROM code after all...
000975r 3               ;
000975r 3               ;
000975r 3               .debuginfo
000975r 3               .setcpu "65C02"
000975r 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000975r 3               ;  macros in hyforth.s
000975r 3               ;.macro WCRLF_np                 -- print CRLF, no push/pull of A
000975r 3               ;.macro WCRLF                    -- print CRLF, p/p A
000975r 3               ;.macro WSEQ_np  strlbl          -- print (strlbl) + \0, p/p Y only
000975r 3               ;.macro WSEQ  strlbl             -- print (strlbl) + \0, p/p Y and A, w/CRLF
000975r 3               ;.macro ANSI b1,b2,b3,b4,b5,b6   -- print up to six ANSI codes, p/p A
000975r 3               ;
000975r 3               ;.macro ERR_entry errmsg         -- macro to create errmsg entry
000975r 3               ;.macro WERR errptr              -- write error using
000975r 3               ;
000975r 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000975r 3               ;  definitions in hyforth.s
000975r 3               ;
000975r 3               ; DEBUG := 1        ; enable inclusion of debug code
000975r 3               ;
000975r 3               ; INROM = $A000
000975r 3               ;
000975r 3               ; malloc stack and managed memory area
000975r 3               ; both move downwards like other stacks
000975r 3               ;
000975r 3               ;MEMEND = $01FE            ; pointers DOWN from MEMEND+MEMSTK (store-and-decrement)
000975r 3               ;MEMTOP = $8000            ; malloc allocates DOWN from MEMTOP
000975r 3               ;
000975r 3               ;INBUF = $0400             ; see stacks segment
000975r 3               ;
000975r 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000975r 3               ;  HyFedit definitions
000975r 3               ;
000975r 3               HFE_EOL = $00              ; end of line
000975r 3               HFE_EOR = $09              ; tab means end of row
000975r 3               HFE_HZC = $2D              ; for horizontal frame '-'
000975r 3               HFE_VTC = $7C              ; for vertical frame   '|'
000975r 3               ;
000975r 3               ;
000975r 3               ;.segment "ZEROPAGE"
000975r 3               ; **** hyforth.s ZP
000975r 3               ;
000975r 3               ;.org $C0
000975r 3               ;HFE_BPTR:   .res 2             ; pointer into 1K buffer      $C0
000975r 3               ;HFE_CH:     .res 1             ; cursor horizontal           $C2
000975r 3               ;HFE_CV:     .res 1             ; cursor vertical             $C3
000975r 3               ;HFE_T0:     .res 1             ; reserved                    $C4
000975r 3               ;HFE_T0H:    .res 1             ; reserved                    $C5
000975r 3               ;HFE_T1:     .res 1             ; reserved                    $C6
000975r 3               ;HFE_T1H:    .res 1             ; reserved                    $C7
000975r 3               ;
000975r 3               ;TEMP0:     .res 2             ;  DUMPREG                    $C8
000975r 3               ;TEMP8:     .res 2             ;  Hstring macro              $CA
000975r 3               ;TEMP9:     .res 2             ;  Imm                        $CC
000975r 3               ;MEMPTR:    .res 2             ;  malloc                     $CE
000975r 3               ;MEMLAST:   .res 2             ;  malloc                     $D0
000975r 3               ;TIB:       .res 2             ; pointer to input buffer     $D2
000975r 3               ;TIBEND:    .res 2             ; pointer to end of TIB       $D4
000975r 3               ;DFLAG:     .res 1             ; debug flag                  $D6
000975r 3               ;ERRFLAG:   .res 1             ; error type, 0 = none        $D7
000975r 3               ;ERRPTR:    .res 2             ; ptr to mitigation/message   $D8
000975r 3               ;TEMP1:     .res 2     ; first, COPYTORAM, MEMCPY            $F0
000975r 3               ;TEMP2:     .res 2     ; second, COPYTORAM, MEMCPY           $F2
000975r 3               ;TEMP3:     .res 2     ; third, COPYTORAM, MEMCPY            $F4
000975r 3               ;TEMP4:     .res 2     ; fourth,                             $F6
000975r 3               ;NXTTOK:    .res 2     ; next token in tib (INBUF)           $F8
000975r 3               ;TEMP5:     .res 1     ;    WORDS, WFIND, MEMCPY             $FC
000975r 3               ;TEMP6:     .res 1     ;    WORDS, DIGCONT, TEXTGET          $FD
000975r 3               ;TEMP7:     .res 2     ;    AUTOLOAD                         $FE
000975r 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000975r 3               ;
000975r 3               ;     hyforth.s buffers
000975r 3               ;.segment "BUFFERS"
000975r 3               ;
000975r 3               ;   *** at $0400
000975r 3               ;INBUF:
000975r 3               ;      .res 256
000975r 3               ;
000975r 3               ;   *** at $0600
000975r 3               ;MEMSTK:                      ; memory manager
000975r 3               ;      .res 510
000975r 3               ;      .res 2
000975r 3               ;
000975r 3               ;
000975r 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000975r 3               ;  hyforth.s starts at $0800
000975r 3               ;
000975r 3               ;  space reserved for *stuff* at $0806 to $0825
000975r 3               ;
000975r 3               ;HFE_RSV:           ; at $0806
000975r 3               ;HFECOLS:        ; offsets to data in RAM
000975r 3               ;    .byte $3F    ; 63 columns w/ $00 or $09 in rightmost
000975r 3               ;HFEROWS:
000975r 3               ;    .byte $10    ; 16 rows
000975r 3               ;HFEBUF:
000975r 3               ;    .res 2       ; addr to buffer
000975r 3               ;             ideally, we would allocate 1K with 'malloc', then copy
000975r 3               ;             the address to HFEBUF in HyForth, then call HFE_COLD
000975r 3               ;             ...or if buffer already got stuff, call HFE_WARM
000975r 3               ;
000975r 3               ;   .res 6
000975r 3               ;HFE_TITLE:
000975r 3               ;    .byte "Generic ScreenX"
000975r 3               ;    .byte 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0    ; title of screen - zero terminated string, up to 12 chars incl \0
000975r 3               ;
000975r 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000975r 3               ;.segment "FORTH_EDIT"
000975r 3               ;                             ; this will be at $F500 until further notice...
000975r 3               HFE_WARM:
000975r 3  A9 01          lda #1                      ; set 'warm start' flag, HFE_T0H
000977r 3  85 C5          sta HFE_T0H
000979r 3  80 02          bra hfe_warmover                    ; skip over next inst
00097Br 3               HFE_COLD:
00097Br 3  64 C5          stz HFE_T0H
00097Dr 3               hfe_warmover:
00097Dr 3  AD rr rr       lda HFEBUF+1
000980r 3  85 C1          sta HFE_BPTR+1
000982r 3  20 rr rr     jsr WRITE_BYTE
000985r 3  AD rr rr       lda HFEBUF                   ; find out where screen buffer is
000988r 3  85 C0          sta HFE_BPTR
00098Ar 3  20 rr rr     jsr WRITE_BYTE
00098Dr 3  20 rr rr       jsr HFE_SCRINIT
000990r 3  64 C2          stz HFE_CH                  ; set cursor position
000992r 3  64 C3          stz HFE_CV
000994r 3  4C rr rr       jmp HFE_START
000997r 3               
000997r 3               HFE_START:
000997r 3  20 rr rr       jsr  HFE_SCRINIT
00099Ar 3  20 rr rr       jsr  HFE_REDRAW
00099Dr 3               
00099Dr 3               HFE_PARSE:
00099Dr 3               
00099Dr 3               hfe_getline:
00099Dr 3               
00099Dr 3               HFE_EXIT:
00099Dr 3  60             rts
00099Er 3               ;
00099Er 3               ; end of main HyFedit code
00099Er 3               ;
00099Er 3               ;-------------------------------------------
00099Er 3               ;   Screen Init
00099Er 3               HFE_SCRINIT:
00099Er 3                 ;jsr ANSICLS
00099Er 3  A9 40          lda #$40         ; 64 columns, including line ending
0009A0r 3  8D rr rr       sta HFECOLS
0009A3r 3  A9 10          lda #$10         ; 16 rows
0009A5r 3  8D rr rr       sta HFEROWS
0009A8r 3  60             rts
0009A9r 3               
0009A9r 3               ;-------------------------------------------
0009A9r 3               ;   Redraw
0009A9r 3               HFE_REDRAW:
0009A9r 3  20 rr rr       jsr hfe_draw_dline                     ; draw top line
0009ACr 3  20 rr rr       jsr hfe_draw_title                     ; draw title line
0009AFr 3  20 rr rr       jsr hfe_draw_dline                     ; draw border
0009B2r 3  AE rr rr       ldx HFEROWS
0009B5r 3  A9 30          lda #$30
0009B7r 3  85 C6          sta HFE_T1                              ; for line #
0009B9r 3               HFE_LP0:
0009B9r 3  20 rr rr       jsr hfe_draw_bline
0009BCr 3               ;  iny
0009BCr 3               ;  tya
0009BCr 3  AD rr rr       lda HFECOLS
0009BFr 3  18             clc
0009C0r 3  65 C0          adc HFE_BPTR                           ; update pointer to get to
0009C2r 3  85 C0          sta HFE_BPTR
0009C4r 3  90 02          bcc HFE_SK0                            ; next line.
0009C6r 3  E6 C1          inc HFE_BPTR + 1
0009C8r 3               HFE_SK0:
0009C8r 3  A5 C6          lda HFE_T1                             ; for printed line count
0009CAr 3  1A             inc a                                  ; increment for next
0009CBr 3  C9 3A          cmp #$3A                               ; line, and see if need
0009CDr 3  D0 03          bne HFE_SK1                            ; alpha hex yet.
0009CFr 3  18             clc
0009D0r 3  69 07          adc #7                                 ; yup.  It's now 'A'
0009D2r 3               HFE_SK1:
0009D2r 3  85 C6          sta HFE_T1
0009D4r 3  CA             dex
0009D5r 3  D0 E2          bne HFE_LP0
0009D7r 3  20 rr rr       jsr hfe_draw_dline                     ; draw bottom line
0009DAr 3  60             rts
0009DBr 3               ;
0009DBr 3               ;  *** draw horizontal line
0009DBr 3               ;
0009DBr 3               hfe_draw_dline:
0009DBr 3  AD rr rr       lda HFECOLS
0009DEr 3  69 03          adc #3
0009E0r 3  AA             tax
0009E1r 3               hfe_ddloop:
0009E1r 3  A9 2D          lda #'-'
0009E3r 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR
0009E6r 3  CA             dex
0009E7r 3  D0 F8          bne hfe_ddloop
0009E9r 3  A9 0D 20 rr    WCRLF_np                      ; CRLF w/o push/pull of A
0009EDr 3  rr A9 0A 20  
0009F1r 3  rr rr        
0009F3r 3  60             rts
0009F4r 3               ;
0009F4r 3               ;  *** draw title line
0009F4r 3               ;
0009F4r 3               hfe_draw_title:
0009F4r 3  A9 7C          lda #'|'
0009F6r 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR
0009F9r 3  A0 14          ldy #20
0009FBr 3  AE rr rr       ldx HFECOLS
0009FEr 3  E8             inx
0009FFr 3  A9 20          lda #$20
000A01r 3               hfe_dtloop0:
000A01r 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR
000A04r 3  CA             dex
000A05r 3  88             dey
000A06r 3  D0 F9          bne hfe_dtloop0
000A08r 3                 ;
000A08r 3  A9 31          lda #$31              ; set color to red
000A0Ar 3  85 BA          sta Z_ANSI1
000A0Cr 3  A9 33          lda #$33
000A0Er 3  85 BB          sta Z_ANSI1+1
000A10r 3  20 rr rr       jsr ANSICOL         ; esc[31m
000A13r 3                 ;
000A13r 3  A0 00          ldy #0
000A15r 3  A9 rr          lda #>HFE_TITLE
000A17r 3  85 C5          sta HFE_T0 + 1
000A19r 3  A9 rr          lda #<HFE_TITLE
000A1Br 3  85 C4          sta HFE_T0
000A1Dr 3               hfe_dtloop1:
000A1Dr 3  B1 C4          lda (HFE_T0), y
000A1Fr 3  F0 07          beq hfe_dtcon0
000A21r 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR
000A24r 3  C8             iny
000A25r 3  CA             dex
000A26r 3  80 F5          bra hfe_dtloop1
000A28r 3               hfe_dtcon0:
000A28r 3  64 BA          stz Z_ANSI1         ; set color back to normal
000A2Ar 3  64 BB          stz Z_ANSI1+1
000A2Cr 3  20 rr rr       jsr ANSICOL         ; esc[0m
000A2Fr 3  A9 20          lda #$20
000A31r 3               hfe_dtloop2:
000A31r 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR
000A34r 3  CA             dex
000A35r 3  D0 FA          bne hfe_dtloop2
000A37r 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR
000A3Ar 3  A9 7C          lda #'|'
000A3Cr 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR
000A3Fr 3  A9 0D 20 rr    WCRLF_np                      ; CRLF w/o push/pull of A
000A43r 3  rr A9 0A 20  
000A47r 3  rr rr        
000A49r 3  60             rts
000A4Ar 3               ;
000A4Ar 3               ;  *** draw line from buffer
000A4Ar 3               ;
000A4Ar 3               hfe_draw_bline:
000A4Ar 3  A9 7C          lda #'|'
000A4Cr 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR
000A4Fr 3                 ;
000A4Fr 3  A9 32          lda #$32              ; set color to green
000A51r 3  85 BA          sta Z_ANSI1
000A53r 3  A9 33          lda #$33
000A55r 3  85 BB          sta Z_ANSI1+1
000A57r 3  20 rr rr       jsr ANSICOL
000A5Ar 3                 ;
000A5Ar 3  A5 C6          lda HFE_T1                     ; line #
000A5Cr 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR                 ; print it
000A5Fr 3                 ;
000A5Fr 3  64 BA          stz Z_ANSI1
000A61r 3  64 BB          stz Z_ANSI1+1        ; and back to normal
000A63r 3  20 rr rr       jsr ANSICOL
000A66r 3                 ;
000A66r 3  A9 7C          lda #'|'
000A68r 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR
000A6Br 3  A0 00          ldy #0
000A6Dr 3               hfe_db_loop0:
000A6Dr 3  B1 C0          lda (HFE_BPTR),y                ; if \0 or tab,
000A6Fr 3  F0 1A          beq hfe_db_done                ; line is done
000A71r 3  C9 09          cmp #$09                       ; and we can skip
000A73r 3  F0 16          beq hfe_db_done                ; to next line.
000A75r 3  C9 1F          cmp #$1F
000A77r 3  90 0A          bcc hfe_db_spcsub              ; don't print non-printables
000A79r 3  C9 7F          cmp #$7F                       ;..there SHOULDN'T be any
000A7Br 3  B0 06          bcs hfe_db_spcsub              ; but who knows....
000A7Dr 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR                 ; padding out line.
000A80r 3  C8             iny
000A81r 3  80 EA          bra hfe_db_loop0
000A83r 3               hfe_db_spcsub:
000A83r 3  A9 20          lda #$20
000A85r 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR
000A88r 3  C8             iny
000A89r 3  80 E2          bra hfe_db_loop0               ; substitute spc for non-printables
000A8Br 3               hfe_db_done:
000A8Br 3  84 C7          sty HFE_T1H                    ; swap around to calc extra spaces
000A8Dr 3  AD rr rr       lda HFECOLS
000A90r 3  38             sec
000A91r 3  E5 C7          sbc HFE_T1H
000A93r 3  A8             tay
000A94r 3  A9 20          lda #$20
000A96r 3               hfe_db_loop1:
000A96r 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR                ; pad out rest of line
000A99r 3  88             dey
000A9Ar 3  D0 FA          bne  hfe_db_loop1
000A9Cr 3  A9 7C          lda #'|'
000A9Er 3  20 rr rr       jsr WRITE_CHAR
000AA1r 3  A9 0D 20 rr    WCRLF_np                      ; CRLF w/o push/pull of A
000AA5r 3  rr A9 0A 20  
000AA9r 3  rr rr        
000AABr 3               
000AABr 3               ;  ldy HFE_T1H               ; restore Y and return to calc offset to next line...
000AABr 3               ;  jsr DUMPREG
000AABr 3  60             rts
000AACr 3               
000AACr 3               ;
000AACr 3               ;                 update a line on screen
000AACr 3               ;
000AACr 3               HFE_UPDATE:
000AACr 3  A0 00          ldy #0
000AAEr 3  A2 C3          ldx #HFE_CV
000AB0r 3  A5 C0          lda HFE_BPTR       ; backup HFE_BPTR
000AB2r 3  85 FE          sta HFE_T2          ; same ZP as TEMP7 in HyForth
000AB4r 3  A5 C1          lda HFE_BPTR + 1
000AB6r 3  85 FF          sta HFE_T2+ 1
000AB8r 3               hfe_u_loop0:         ; count zeros/tabs up to current cursor pos
000AB8r 3  B1 C0          lda (HFE_BPTR),y
000ABAr 3  F0 07          beq  HFEU_ZCNT
000ABCr 3  C9 09          cmp #$09
000ABEr 3  F0 03          beq  HFEU_ZCNT
000AC0r 3  C8             iny
000AC1r 3  80 F5          bra hfe_u_loop0
000AC3r 3               HFEU_ZCNT:
000AC3r 3  C8             iny
000AC4r 3  98             tya
000AC5r 3  18             clc
000AC6r 3  65 C0          adc HFE_BPTR         ; update HFE_BPTR
000AC8r 3  90 02          bcc HFEU_SKIP
000ACAr 3  E6 C1          inc HFE_BPTR + 1
000ACCr 3               HFEU_SKIP:
000ACCr 3  A0 00          ldy #0
000ACEr 3  8A             txa
000ACFr 3  F0 03          beq HFEU_LFOUND
000AD1r 3  CA             dex
000AD2r 3  80 E4          bra hfe_u_loop0
000AD4r 3               HFEU_LFOUND:
000AD4r 3  A5 C2          lda HFE_CH
000AD6r 3  85 C6          sta HFE_T1           ; backup horizontal cursor pos
000AD8r 3  A9 00          lda #0
000ADAr 3  85 C2          sta HFE_CH          ; cuz we wanna draw from beginning of line
000ADCr 3  20 rr rr       jsr hfe_place_cur   ; place the cursor
000ADFr 3  20 rr rr       jsr hfe_draw_bline  ; and draw from the current HFE_PTR until line end
000AE2r 3  A5 FE          lda HFE_T2
000AE4r 3  85 C0          sta HFE_BPTR
000AE6r 3  A5 FF          lda HFE_T2 + 1       ; restore HFE_PTR and HFE_CH
000AE8r 3  85 C0          sta HFE_BPTR
000AEAr 3  A5 C6          lda HFE_T1
000AECr 3  85 C2          sta HFE_CH
000AEEr 3  60             rts
000AEFr 3               
000AEFr 3               hfe_place_cur:
000AEFr 3  A5 C2          lda HFE_CH
000AF1r 3  18             clc
000AF2r 3  69 02          adc #2
000AF4r 3  85 F4          sta TEMP3
000AF6r 3  A5 C3          lda HFE_CV
000AF8r 3  18             clc
000AF9r 3  69 04          adc #4
000AFBr 3  85 F6          sta TEMP4
000AFDr 3  20 rr rr       jsr ANSISCR
000B00r 3  60             rts
000B01r 3               ;
000B01r 3               ; end of hyf_edit.S
000B01r 3               ;
000B01r 3               
000B01r 2               HFE_EDIT_END:
000B01r 2               ;
000B01r 2               ;
000B01r 2               ROMCODEEND:                         ; end of all code
000B01r 2               ;
000B01r 2               ;  end of hyforth.s
000B01r 2               ;
000B01r 2               ;------------------------------------------------------------------------
000B01r 2               .segment "FORTH_PAGED_ROM"
000000r 2               ;------------------------------------------------------------------------
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ;    hyf_rom.s -- COPYTORAM
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               .include "hyf_rom.s"
000000r 3               ;
000000r 3               ;  COPYTORAM  - This HAS to sit at $A000 or the HyForth engine in OSROM will NOT find it.
000000r 3               ;    so....this always needs to be the first thing in the library image before the heap,
000000r 3               ;    therefore .org $A000, .include 'hyf_rom.s', .org $0800, ...(heap).
000000r 3               ;
000000r 3               COPYMAIN = COPYSTART
000000r 3               RAMST = RAMSTART
000000r 3               COPYENDS = ends - RAMSTART + COPYSTART
000000r 3               
000000r 3               COPYTORAM:
000000r 3  4C rr rr         jmp CTRSTART                     ; copies from mainoff thru endsoff to ramstart
000003r 3  rr               .byte >h_exit
000004r 3  rr               .byte <h_exit
000005r 3  rr               .byte >ends
000006r 3               CTRSTART:
000006r 3               ;
000006r 3               ;    the 10-23-26 1920 version
000006r 3               ;
000006r 3               ;    reworked to used ZP_TEMP_VEC, ZP_TEMP_VEC2, ZP_TEMP_VEC3
000006r 3               ;                     mainoff      endsoff       ramstart
000006r 3               ;
000006r 3  A9 0D 20 rr      WCRLF_np
00000Ar 3  rr A9 0A 20  
00000Er 3  rr rr        
000010r 3               ;    lda #1
000010r 3               ;    sta supprint               ; supress printing of progress
000010r 3  A9 A1            lda #>COPYMAIN
000012r 3  85 rr            sta ZP_TEMP_VEC+1
000014r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_BYTE
000017r 3  A9 00            lda #<COPYMAIN
000019r 3  85 rr            sta ZP_TEMP_VEC
00001Br 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_BYTE
00001Er 3  A9 2B            lda #'+'
000020r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
000023r 3  A9 9B            lda #<COPYENDS
000025r 3  85 rr            sta ZP_TEMP_VEC2
000027r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_BYTE
00002Ar 3  A9 B5            lda #>COPYENDS
00002Cr 3  85 rr            sta ZP_TEMP_VEC2+1
00002Er 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_BYTE
000031r 3                   ;
000031r 3  A9 rr            lda #>RAMST
000033r 3  85 rr            sta ZP_TEMP_VEC3+1
000035r 3  A9 rr            lda #<RAMST
000037r 3  85 rr            sta ZP_TEMP_VEC3
000039r 3  20 rr rr         jsr MEMCPY
00003Cr 3  A9 20            lda #$20
00003Er 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
000041r 3  A9 66            lda #'f'
000043r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
000046r 3  A9 69            lda #'i'
000048r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
00004Br 3  A9 6E            lda #'n'
00004Dr 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
000050r 3  A9 0D 20 rr      WCRLF_np
000054r 3  rr A9 0A 20  
000058r 3  rr rr        
00005Ar 3  60               rts
00005Br 3               
00005Br 3               ;     the 10-22-26 1922 version
00005Br 3               ;
00005Br 3               ;    WCRLF_np
00005Br 3               ;    WCRLF_np
00005Br 3               ;    stz supprint               ; let it print
00005Br 3               ;    lda #>COPYMAIN
00005Br 3               ;    sta ZP_TEMP_VEC+1
00005Br 3               ;    lda #<COPYMAIN
00005Br 3               ;    sta ZP_TEMP_VEC
00005Br 3                   ;
00005Br 3               ;    lda #<COPYENDS
00005Br 3               ;    sta ZP_TEMP_VEC2
00005Br 3               ;    lda #>COPYENDS
00005Br 3               ;    sta ZP_TEMP_VEC2+1
00005Br 3                   ;
00005Br 3               ;    lda #>RAMST
00005Br 3               ;    sta ZP_TEMP_VEC3+1
00005Br 3               ;    lda #<RAMST
00005Br 3               ;    sta ZP_TEMP_VEC3
00005Br 3               ;    WCRLF_np
00005Br 3               ;    jsr MEMCPY
00005Br 3               ;    rts
00005Br 3               ;;
00005Br 3               
00005Br 2               ;
00005Br 2               ;
00005Br 2               COPYSTART := $A100              ; marks beginning of copy in ROM space
00005Br 2               ;-----------------------------------------------------------------------
00005Br 2               ;           BELOW ENDS UP IN RAM after COPYTORAM has run
00005Br 2               ;-----------------------------------------------------------------------
00005Br 2               .segment "FORTH_CODE"
000000r 2               RAMSTART:
000000r 2  4C rr rr         jmp forth_main             ; MAIN program start
000003r 2  4C rr rr         jmp reset
000006r 2               ;
000006r 2               ;        32 bytes reserved for HyFedit (see hyf_edit.s)
000006r 2               ;
000006r 2               HFE_RSV:
000006r 2               HFECOLS:        ; offsets to data in RAM
000006r 2  40               .byte $40    ; 64 columns w/ $00 or $09 in rightmost
000007r 2               HFEROWS:
000007r 2  10               .byte $10    ; 16 rows
000008r 2               HFEBUF:
000008r 2  xx xx            .res 2       ; addr to buffer
00000Ar 2  xx xx xx xx      .res 6
00000Er 2  xx xx        
000010r 2               HFE_TITLE:
000010r 2  3C 2D            .byte "<-"
000012r 2               HFE_TTXT:
000012r 2  47 65 6E 65      .byte "Generic ScreenXX"
000016r 2  72 69 63 20  
00001Ar 2  53 63 72 65  
000022r 2  2D 3E            .byte "->"
000024r 2  00 00            .byte 0, 0
000026r 2               ;--------------------------------------------------------------------
000026r 2               ;      library jump table
000026r 2               ;
000026r 2               ;    see ftrain.s and bload.s for examples of library format details
000026r 2               ;
000026r 2               LASTLIB = 4
000026r 2               lib_jumptable:
000026r 2  A8 B5            LIBENTRY (LT_FTRAIN + COPYSTART - RAMSTART)
000028r 2  17 B9            LIBENTRY (LT_ANSI + COPYSTART - RAMSTART)
00002Ar 2  A2 BA            LIBENTRY (LT_RAMTEST + COPYSTART - RAMSTART)
00002Cr 2  EA BB            LIBENTRY (LT_BLOAD + COPYSTART - RAMSTART)
00002Er 2  48 BB            LIBENTRY (LT_EDITTEST + COPYSTART - RAMSTART)
000030r 2  00 00 00 00      .byte 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0   ; extra padding, dynamically add more libraries
000034r 2  00 00        
000036r 2  00 00 00 00      .byte 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0
00003Ar 2  00 00        
00003Cr 2  00 00 00 00      .byte 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0
000040r 2  00 00        
000042r 2  00 00            .byte 0, 0
000044r 2               ;
000044r 2               ;
000044r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000044r 2               ;        primitives.s -- original AGSB hardcoded dictionary
000044r 2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
000044r 2               ; ( -- ) ae exit forth
000044r 2  00 00 03 62  def_word "bye", "bye", 0
000048r 2  79 65        
00004Ar 2  4C rr rr         jmp MON_START               ; jump to WOZMON
00004Dr 2               ;
00004Dr 2               ; ( -- ) ae abort
00004Dr 2  rr rr 05 61  def_word "abort", "abort_", 0
000051r 2  62 6F 72 74  
000055r 2  4C rr rr         jmp abort
000058r 2               ;
000058r 2               ; ( -- ) resets buffer to INBUF
000058r 2  rr rr 05 72  def_word "reset", "reset_", 0
00005Cr 2  65 73 65 74  
000060r 2  4C rr rr         jmp reset
000063r 2               ;
000063r 2               ;
000063r 2               ;------------ CRITICAL CORE PRIMITIVES (AGSB and PGS) ----------------
000063r 2               ;   (the 'primitive' primitives)
000063r 2               ;
000063r 2               ;   COMPILE (:), FINISH (;), FETCH (@), STORE (!)
000063r 2               ;      including:  keeps, this, next, finish
000063r 2               ;       ...and other stuff.
000063r 2               ;
000063r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000063r 2               ; ( a -- ) execute a jump to a reference at top of data stack
000063r 2  rr rr 04 65  def_word "exec", "exec", 0
000067r 2  78 65 63     
00006Ar 2  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0
00006Dr 2  6C F0 00         jmp (TEMP1)        ; assumes an address on top of DS
000070r 2               
000070r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000070r 2               ; ( -- ) execute a jump to a reference at IP
000070r 2  rr rr 02 3A  def_word ":$", "docode", 0
000074r 2  24           
000075r 2  6C EC 00         jmp (INSTPTR)                 ;  DOCODE (thus the ':')
000078r 2               
000078r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000078r 2               ; ( -- ) execute a jump to next
000078r 2  rr rr 02 3B  def_word ";$", "donext", 0        ;  'next' (thus the ';')
00007Cr 2  24           
00007Dr 2  4C rr rr         jmp next
000080r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000080r 2               ;
000080r 2               ; ( w a -- ) ; [a] = w    (w is word, a is an address)
000080r 2  rr rr 01 21  def_word "!", "store", 0
000084r 2               storew:
000084r 2  20 rr rr         jsr spull_1             ; get address, store in TEMP2
000087r 2  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0             ; get data, store in TEMP1
00008Ar 2  A2 F2            ldx #TEMP2              ;  [a]
00008Cr 2  A0 F0            ldy #TEMP1              ;   w
00008Er 2  20 rr rr         jsr copyinto            ; copy TEMP2 stuff to addr in TEMP1 (opposite of @)..
000091r 2  4C rr rr         jmp next                ;            ...(see below)
000094r 2               ;
000094r 2               ;--------------------------------------------FETCH--------------------
000094r 2               ; ( a -- w ) ; w = [a]
000094r 2  rr rr 01 40  def_word "@", "fetch", 0      ; replace addr of data on top of DS, with data pointed to
000098r 2               fetchw:
000098r 2  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0             ; get addr from DS
00009Br 2  A2 F0            ldx #TEMP1
00009Dr 2  A0 F2            ldy #TEMP2
00009Fr 2  20 rr rr         jsr copyfrom            ; copies data from [TEMP1] => TEMP2
0000A2r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0000A2r 2               ;            NEXT entry point for many AGSB primitives, and
0000A2r 2               ;            almost all other hard-coded words.
0000A2r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0000A2r 2                                           ; in fall through, this copies TEMP2 (in y) to TEMP1,
0000A2r 2                                           ; overwriting the address that was pulled from the stack.
0000A2r 2                                           ;
0000A2r 2                                           ; in theory, one could load y with any TEMPx and
0000A2r 2                                           ; call jump to 'copys' to finish off to 'next'.
0000A2r 2                                           ; in PRACTICE, AGSB didn't reuse this much.
0000A2r 2                                           ;
0000A2r 2               copys:                      ; copy from cell at y (zp) to TEMP1
0000A2r 2  B9 00 00         lda 0, y
0000A5r 2  85 F0            sta TEMP1
0000A7r 2  B9 01 00         lda 1, y
0000AAr 2                                           ; 'keeps' is a shortcut to the 'second half' of the
0000AAr 2                                           ; (sta TEMP1) routine to return results; to push another
0000AAr 2                                           ; variable on the stack, do it explicitly.
0000AAr 2                                           ;
0000AAr 2                                           ; 'this' assumes 'keeps' has already been done.
0000AAr 2                                           ;  Almost every hard-coded word uses keeps, this, or next
0000AAr 2                                           ;  to end a word; 'next' ends with 'exit' of course.
0000AAr 2                                           ;
0000AAr 2               keeps:                      ; saves bytes since have to get here anyway
0000AAr 2  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
0000ACr 2               this:                       ; same as above
0000ACr 2  20 rr rr         jsr spush_0             ; then push back on stack
0000AFr 2  4C rr rr         jmp next
0000B2r 2               ;
0000B2r 2               ;-----------------------IMMEDIATE, '[', ']', ','-----------------------
0000B2r 2  rr rr 01 49  def_word "I", "Imm", 0
0000B6r 2               wimm:                        ; jmp here if proccessing a compiled
0000B6r 2  A5 E5            lda LASTHEAP+1           ;   word that needs to run 'immediate'.
0000B8r 2  85 CD            sta TEMP9+1
0000BAr 2  A5 E4            lda LASTHEAP             ; get addr of 'last' compiled word, copy to TEMP4, add 2
0000BCr 2  18               clc
0000BDr 2  69 02            adc #2                   ; ..to find where length byte is...
0000BFr 2  85 CC            sta TEMP9
0000C1r 2  90 02            bcc IMMSKIP
0000C3r 2  E6 CD            inc TEMP9+1
0000C5r 2               IMMSKIP:
0000C5r 2  A0 00            ldy #0
0000C7r 2  B1 CC            lda (TEMP9),y
0000C9r 2  09 80            ora #$80                 ; ...set bit 7 and store
0000CBr 2  91 CC            sta (TEMP9),y
0000CDr 2  4C rr rr         jmp next
0000D0r 2               ;
0000D0r 2  rr rr 81 5B  def_word "[", "leftbrack", FLAG_IMM       ; switch to 'interpret'
0000D4r 2  64 E0            stz STATUS
0000D6r 2  4C rr rr         jmp next
0000D9r 2               ;
0000D9r 2  rr rr 01 5D  def_word "]", "rtbrack", 0               ; switch back to 'compile'
0000DDr 2  A9 01            lda #1
0000DFr 2  85 E0            sta STATUS
0000E1r 2  4C rr rr         jmp next
0000E4r 2               ;
0000E4r 2               ;
0000E4r 2                                                        ; NOTE: 'lit' or similar will have pushed a value
0000E4r 2                                                        ; or address onto stack; 'comma' stores it inline in word
0000E4r 2                                                        ;
0000E4r 2  rr rr 01 2C  def_word ",", "xcomma", 0                ; pull data from top of stack, store at 'here' (NEXTHEAP)
0000E8r 2                                                        ; , increment 'here' to point to next cell.
0000E8r 2  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0                          ;
0000EBr 2  A0 00            ldy #0                               ;
0000EDr 2  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
0000EFr 2  91 E6            sta (NEXTHEAP),y                     ; POP DS TO TEMP1, [here] = TEMP1
0000F1r 2  C8               iny
0000F2r 2  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
0000F4r 2  91 E6            sta (NEXTHEAP),y
0000F6r 2  A2 E6            ldx #NEXTHEAP                        ; here += 2
0000F8r 2  A9 02            lda #2
0000FAr 2  20 rr rr         jsr addwx
0000FDr 2  4C rr rr         jmp next
000100r 2               
000100r 2               ;--------------------------------------------SEMIS-----------------
000100r 2  rr rr 81 3B  def_word ";", "semis",  FLAG_IMM
000104r 2  A5 FA            lda BACKHEAP
000106r 2  85 E4            sta LASTHEAP                ; bring back BACKHEAP to LASTHEAP
000108r 2  A5 FB            lda BACKHEAP + 1
00010Ar 2  85 E5            sta LASTHEAP + 1
00010Cr 2               
00010Cr 2  64 E0            stz STATUS                  ; set status to 'interpret' (presumably from 'compile')
00010Er 2               
00010Er 2               finish:                         ; compiled words must end with exit
00010Er 2  A9 rr            lda #<exit
000110r 2  85 EE            sta WORKREG
000112r 2  A9 rr            lda #>exit
000114r 2  85 EF            sta WORKREG + 1
000116r 2  20 rr rr         jsr wcomma                  ; change NEXTHEAP to point to addr of 'exit',
000119r 2                                               ; and make sure is last entry in code table for word...
000119r 2                                               ; as all good Forth compiled words should do.
000119r 2  4C rr rr         jmp next
00011Cr 2               ;
00011Cr 2               ;
00011Cr 2               ;------------------------------------------COMPILE------------------
00011Cr 2  rr rr 01 3A  def_word ":", "colon", 0
000120r 2  A5 E6            lda NEXTHEAP
000122r 2  85 FA            sta BACKHEAP                ; backup NEXTHEAP to BACKHEAP
000124r 2  A5 E7            lda NEXTHEAP + 1
000126r 2  85 FB            sta BACKHEAP + 1
000128r 2               
000128r 2  A9 01            lda #1                      ; set status to 'compile'
00012Ar 2  85 E0            sta STATUS
00012Cr 2               
00012Cr 2               COMPHEADER:
00012Cr 2               ; copy LASTHEAP into (NEXTHEAP)
00012Cr 2  A0 E4            ldy #LASTHEAP
00012Er 2  20 rr rr         jsr comma                    ; change NEXTHEAP to point to LASTHEAP  ('here' <= 'last')
000131r 2  20 rr rr         jsr token                    ; get first token, the name of new word
000134r 2  A0 00            ldy #0                       ; copy it to heap: length and name
000136r 2                                                ; code field comes with later proc
000136r 2               COMPLOOP:
000136r 2  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK), y
000138r 2  C9 20            cmp #$20
00013Ar 2  F0 0B            beq COMPEND
00013Cr 2  C0 00            cpy #0                       ; if this is the length field...
00013Er 2  D0 02            bne COMCOPY
000140r 2  09 40            ora #$40                     ; set bit six for 'compiled' word
000142r 2               COMCOPY:
000142r 2  91 E6            sta (NEXTHEAP), y
000144r 2  C8               iny
000145r 2  D0 EF            bne COMPLOOP
000147r 2               COMPEND:
000147r 2  98               tya                          ; and update NEXTHEAP  :  'here' incremented by length
000148r 2  A2 E6            ldx #(NEXTHEAP)
00014Ar 2  20 rr rr         jsr addwx                   ; 'here' now at CFA
00014Dr 2               
00014Dr 2               ;~~~~~~~~ all done....
00014Dr 2  4C rr rr         jmp next                     ; and then see below; compiled word will continue until ';'
000150r 2               ;
000150r 2               ;
000150r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000150r 2               ;               must have words...
000150r 2               ;
000150r 2               primitives:
000150r 2               .include "primitives.s"
000150r 3               ;
000150r 3               ; primitives.s
000150r 3               ;
000150r 3               
000150r 3               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
000150r 3               ;  library management
000150r 3               ;
000150r 3  rr rr 06 6C  def_word "libcnt", "libcnt", 0
000154r 3  69 62 63 6E  
000158r 3  74           
000159r 3  20 rr rr         jsr LIBCNT
00015Cr 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
00015Er 3  64 F1            stz TEMP1+1
000160r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_0
000163r 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
000166r 3               
000166r 3               LIBCNT:
000166r 3  A9 rr            lda #>lib_jumptable
000168r 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
00016Ar 3  A9 rr            lda #<lib_jumptable
00016Cr 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
00016Er 3  A0 FF            ldy #$FF
000170r 3               LIBCNTLOOP:
000170r 3  C8               iny
000171r 3  B1 F0            lda (TEMP1),y
000173r 3  C8               iny
000174r 3  11 F0            ora (TEMP1),y          ; entry after last should be $0000
000176r 3  D0 F8            bne LIBCNTLOOP
000178r 3  98               tya
000179r 3  4A               lsr
00017Ar 3  60               rts
00017Br 3               
00017Br 3  rr rr 03 6C  def_word "lib", "lib", 0
00017Fr 3  69 62        
000181r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_2                 ; nth library
000184r 3  A9 rr            lda #>lib_jumptable
000186r 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
000188r 3  A6 F4            ldx TEMP3               ; check that TEMP3 is not neg or out of bounds
00018Ar 3  CA               dex                     ; need it 0 - LASTLIB-1 indexed
00018Br 3  30 1B            bmi LIBNG
00018Dr 3                   ;
00018Dr 3                   ;
00018Dr 3  20 rr rr         jsr GETLIBENTRY
000190r 3  98               tya
000191r 3  D0 15            bne LIBNG
000193r 3               LIBWLOOP:
000193r 3  B1 C8            lda (TEMP0),y      ; TEMP0 should point at name of entry, step through
000195r 3  F0 03            beq LIBWEND       ; entry via y
000197r 3  C8               iny
000198r 3  80 F9            bra LIBWLOOP       ; step through name
00019Ar 3               LIBWEND:
00019Ar 3  C8               iny                 ; jump past lib type 'C' or 'B'
00019Br 3  C8               iny
00019Cr 3  B1 C8            lda (TEMP0),y
00019Er 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1                 ; hi byte of lib address
0001A0r 3  C8               iny
0001A1r 3  B1 C8            lda (TEMP0),y
0001A3r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1                  ; lo byte of lib address
0001A5r 3               LIBEND:
0001A5r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_0               ; should have address of first entry of lib n in TEMP1
0001A8r 3               LIBNG:
0001A8r 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
0001ABr 3               
0001ABr 3  rr rr 04 6C  def_word "libs", "libs", 0
0001AFr 3  69 62 73     
0001B2r 3  20 rr rr         jsr print_libtable
0001B5r 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
0001B8r 3               
0001B8r 3               print_libtable:
0001B8r 3  A9 rr            lda #>lib_jumptable
0001BAr 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
0001BCr 3  A2 00            ldx #0
0001BEr 3               LIBSLOOP:
0001BEr 3  20 rr rr         jsr GETLIBENTRY    ; looks up lib entry #x, sets up TEMP1 and TEMP0, y = 0
0001C1r 3  98               tya
0001C2r 3  D0 35            bne LIBSEND
0001C4r 3               LIBSWLOOP:
0001C4r 3  B1 C8            lda (TEMP0),y      ; TEMP0 should point at name of entry, step through
0001C6r 3  F0 06            beq LIBSWEND       ; entry via y
0001C8r 3  C8               iny
0001C9r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR     ; prints name of library
0001CCr 3  80 F6            bra LIBSWLOOP
0001CEr 3               LIBSWEND:
0001CEr 3  A9 2D            lda #'-'
0001D0r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
0001D3r 3  C8               iny
0001D4r 3  B1 C8            lda (TEMP0),y
0001D6r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR                   ; print type 'C' or 'B'
0001D9r 3  A9 20            lda #$20
0001DBr 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_CHAR
0001DEr 3  C8               iny
0001DFr 3  B1 C8            lda (TEMP0),y
0001E1r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_BYTE                   ; hi byte of lib address
0001E4r 3  C8               iny
0001E5r 3  B1 C8            lda (TEMP0),y
0001E7r 3  20 rr rr         jsr WRITE_BYTE                   ; lo byte of lib address
0001EAr 3  A9 0D 20 rr      WCRLF_np
0001EEr 3  rr A9 0A 20  
0001F2r 3  rr rr        
0001F4r 3  E8               inx
0001F5r 3  F0 02            beq LIBSEND
0001F7r 3  80 C5            bra LIBSLOOP
0001F9r 3               LIBSEND:
0001F9r 3  60               rts
0001FAr 3               
0001FAr 3               GETLIBENTRY:               ; get entry pointed to by x
0001FAr 3  8A               txa
0001FBr 3  0A               asl a
0001FCr 3  18               clc
0001FDr 3  69 rr            adc #<lib_jumptable
0001FFr 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
000201r 3  90 02            bcc LIBSNEXT
000203r 3  E6 F1            inc TEMP1+1
000205r 3               LIBSNEXT:
000205r 3  A0 00            ldy #0
000207r 3  B1 F0            lda (TEMP1),y
000209r 3  85 C8            sta TEMP0
00020Br 3  C8               iny
00020Cr 3  B1 F0            lda (TEMP1),y
00020Er 3  85 C9            sta TEMP0+1
000210r 3  88               dey
000211r 3  B1 C8            lda (TEMP0),y
000213r 3  C8               iny
000214r 3  11 C8            ora (TEMP0),y
000216r 3  D0 01            bne LIBSKIP
000218r 3  60               rts
000219r 3               LIBSKIP:
000219r 3  88               dey
00021Ar 3  60               rts
00021Br 3               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
00021Br 3               ; ( -- ) ae list of data stack
00021Br 3  rr rr 02 2E  def_word ".S", "splist", 0      ; changed from %S
00021Fr 3  53           
000220r 3  A5 E8            lda DSPTR
000222r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
000224r 3  A5 E9            lda DSPTR + 1
000226r 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1 + 1
000228r 3  A9 0D 20 rr      WCRLF_np
00022Cr 3  rr A9 0A 20

00052Br 3  80 F6            bra SZLOOP
00052Dr 3               SZEND:
00052Dr 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
000530r 3               ;
000530r 3               ;  print n bytes from addr
000530r 3               ;
000530r 3  rr rr 03 2E  def_word ".sn", "sndot", 0
000534r 3  73 6E

000539r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_1    ; addr to print from
00053Cr 3  A6 F0            ldx TEMP1      ; ignore TEMP+1, 256 bytes max
00053Er 3  A0 00            ldy #0
000540r 3               SNLOOP:

000545r 3  C8               iny
000546r 3  CA               dex
000547r 3  F0 02            beq SNEND
000549r 3  80 F5            bra SNLOOP
00054Br 3               SNEND:
00054Br 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
00054Er 3               ; ( addr n -- w0 w1 ... w(n-1) )  push n words from memory to stack
00054Er 3               ;
00054Er 3  rr rr 07 64  def_word "dsgetn@", "dsgetn", 0
000552r 3  73 67 65 74  
000556r 3  6E 40        
000558r 3  A0 F6            ldy #TEMP4
00055Ar 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull    ; get # WORDS
00055Dr 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_1  ; get ptr addr
000560r 3  20 rr rr         jsr MEMLEN   ; returns TEMP3 w/maddr, length in TEMP1
000563r 3  A5 F6            lda TEMP4
000565r 3  0A               asl a
000566r 3  85 F6            sta TEMP4
000568r 3  C5 F0            cmp TEMP1
00056Ar 3  B0 02            bcs DSGETNSK
00056Cr 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
00056Er 3               DSGETNSK:
00056Er 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
000570r 3  C9 78            cmp #$78

000574r 3  4C rr rr         jmp DSGETERR
000577r 3               DSGETNSK2:
000577r 3  18               clc
000578r 3  69 03            adc #3
00057Ar 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
00057Cr 3  A0 03            ldy #3
00057Er 3  4C rr rr         jmp DSGLOOP
000581r 3               ;
000581r 3               ; ( addr -- w w ... w )  push words from memory to stack
000581r 3               ;
000581r 3  rr rr 06 64  def_word "dsget@", "dsget", 0
000585r 3  73 67 65 74  
000589r 3  40           
00058Ar 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_1    ; get addr on memstack
00058Dr 3  20 rr rr         jsr MEMLEN     ; maddr in TEMP3, length in TEMP1
000590r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
000592r 3  C9 78            cmp #$78
000594r 3  B0 23            bcs DSGETERR
000596r 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
000598r 3  D0 1F            bne DSGETERR    ; too much data, can't push this on
00059Ar 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
00059Cr 3  18               clc
00059Dr 3  69 03            adc #3
00059Fr 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
0005A1r 3  A0 03            ldy #3

0005A5r 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2
0005A7r 3  C8               iny

0005AAr 3  85 F3            sta TEMP2+1
0005ACr 3  C8               iny
0005ADr 3  5A               phy

0005B4r 3  D0 ED            bne DSGLOOP
0005B6r 3               DSGEND:

0005BBr 3  85 D7            sta ERRFLAG
0005BDr 3  4C rr rr         jmp errrtn
0005C0r 3               ;
0005C0r 3               ; ( w w w..len addr -- addr)    stores len BYTES (words * 2) from stack
0005C0r 3               ;
0005C0r 3  rr rr 06 64  def_word "dwstk!", "dwstkstore", 0
0005C4r 3  77 73 74 6B  
0005C8r 3  21

0005CFr 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_1      ; and push ptr addr back on stack
0005D2r 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
0005D5r 3               
0005D5r 3               DW_STFWD:
0005D5r 3  A0 00            ldy #0            ; count up
0005D7r 3               DWFLOOP:

0005D9r 3  38               sec
0005DAr 3  E9 7E            sbc #DSEND
0005DCr 3  B0 13            bcs DW_FWDEND
0005DEr 3  5A               phy
0005DFr 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0
0005E2r 3  7A               ply
0005E3r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
0005E5r 3  91 F4            sta (TEMP3),y
0005E7r 3  C8               iny

0005EAr 3  91 F4            sta (TEMP3),y
0005ECr 3  C8               iny
0005EDr 3  C4 FC            cpy TEMP5
0005EFr 3  D0 E6            bne DWFLOOP
0005F1r 3               DW_FWDEND:
0005F1r 3  60               rts
0005F2r 3               ;
0005F2r 3               ;
0005F2r 3               ; ( 0c 0c 0c...len addr -- addr)    stores len chars from stack
0005F2r 3               ;
0005F2r 3  rr rr 06 64  def_word "dcstk!", "dcstkstore", 0
0005F6r 3  63 73 74 6B  
0005FAr 3  21

000601r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_1      ; and push ptr addr back on stack
000604r 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
000607r 3               
000607r 3               HS_STFWD:
000607r 3  A0 00            ldy #0            ; count up
000609r 3               HSRLOOP:
000609r 3  A5 E8            lda DSPTR
00060Br 3  38               sec
00060Cr 3  E9 7E            sbc #DSEND
00060Er 3  B0 0E            bcs HS_FWDEND
000610r 3  5A               phy
000611r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0
000614r 3  7A               ply
000615r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
000617r 3  91 F4            sta (TEMP3),y
000619r 3  C8               iny
00061Ar 3  C4 FC            cpy TEMP5
00061Cr 3  D0 EB            bne HSRLOOP
00061Er 3               HS_FWDEND:
00061Er 3  60               rts
00061Fr 3               ;
00061Fr 3               ; ( 0c 0c 0c...len addr -- addr)  stores len chars from stack, reverse order
00061Fr 3               ;
00061Fr 3  rr rr 07 72  def_word "rdcstk!", "rdstkstore", 0
000623r 3  64 63 73 74  
000627r 3  6B 21        
000629r 3  20 rr rr         jsr HSSETUP
00062Cr 3  20 rr rr         jsr HS_STREV
00062Fr 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_1      ; and push ptr addr back on stack
000632r 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
000635r 3               
000635r 3               HSSETUP:
000635r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_1     ; get addr from malloc run -> TEMP2
000638r 3  20 rr rr         jsr MEMLEN      ; length in TEMP1, maddr TEMP3
00063Br 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
00063Dr 3  85 FC            sta TEMP5
00063Fr 3  A5 F4            lda TEMP3
000641r 3  18               clc
000642r 3  69 03            adc #3          ; calc offset to storage
000644r 3  85 F4            sta TEMP3
000646r 3  90 02            bcc HSSETUPEND
000648r 3  E6 F5            inc TEMP3+1
00064Ar 3               HSSETUPEND:
00064Ar 3  60               rts
00064Br 3               
00064Br 3               HS_STREV:
00064Br 3  A4 FC            ldy TEMP5            ; count down from length
00064Dr 3  A9 00            lda #0
00064Fr 3  88               dey
000650r 3  91 F4            sta (TEMP3),y
000652r 3               HSFLOOP:
000652r 3  A5 E8            lda DSPTR
000654r 3  38               sec
000655r 3  E9 7E            sbc #DSEND
000657r 3  B0 0C            bcs HS_REVEND
000659r 3  5A               phy
00065Ar 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0
00065Dr 3  7A               ply
00065Er 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
000660r 3  88               dey
000661r 3  91 F4            sta (TEMP3),y
000663r 3  D0 ED            bne HSFLOOP
000665r 3               HS_REVEND:
000665r 3  60               rts
000666r 3               ;
000666r 3               ;   replace leading zeros with spcs when creating decimal ascii string
000666r 3               ;
000666r 3               DECSFINISH:
000666r 3  A0 00            ldy #0
000668r 3               DECSCLRLOOP:             ; replace $00 or leading $30 with spaces
000668r 3  B1 F4            lda (TEMP3),y
00066Ar 3  F0 06            beq  DECSTSK02
00066Cr 3  C9 30            cmp #'0'
00066Er 3  F0 02            beq  DECSTSK02
000670r 3  80 07            bra  DECSTDONE
000672r 3               DECSTSK02:
000672r 3  A9 20            lda #$20
000674r 3  91 F4            sta (TEMP3),y
000676r 3  C8               iny
000677r 3  80 EF            bra DECSCLRLOOP
000679r 3               DECSTDONE:
000679r 3  60               rts
00067Ar 3               ;
00067Ar 3               ;
00067Ar 3               ;
00067Ar 3  rr rr 05 64  def_word "decs!", "decstore", 0
00067Er 3  65 63 73 21

00068Er 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
000691r 3               ;
000691r 3               ;
000691r 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000691r 3               ;                     ' extensions ' in original AGSB
000691r 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000691r 3               ; ( w n -- w >> n ) -- shift right
000691r 3  rr rr 02 3E  def_word ">>", "shr", 0
000695r 3  3E           
000696r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_1
000699r 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2
00069Br 3  F0 0E            beq SRZERO
00069Dr 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0
0006A0r 3               SRLOOP:
0006A0r 3  46 F1            lsr TEMP1 + 1
0006A2r 3  66 F0            ror TEMP1
0006A4r 3  C6 F2            dec TEMP2
0006A6r 3  D0 F8            bne SRLOOP
0006A8r 3  4C rr rr         jmp this         ; 'this' includes jsr spush_0 and next
0006ABr 3               SRZERO:
0006ABr 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
0006AEr 3               
0006AEr 3               ; ( w n -- w << n ) -- shift left
0006AEr 3  rr rr 02 3C  def_word "<<", "shl", 0
0006B2r 3  3C           
0006B3r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_1
0006B6r 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2
0006B8r 3  F0 0E            beq SLZERO
0006BAr 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0
0006BDr 3               SLLOOP:
0006BDr 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1
0006BFr 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1 + 1
0006C1r 3  C6 F2            dec TEMP2
0006C3r 3  D0 F8            bne SLLOOP
0006C5r 3  4C rr rr         jmp this         ; 'this' includes jsr spush_0 and next
0006C8r 3               SLZERO:
0006C8r 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
0006CBr 3               
0006CBr 3               ;--------------- bit test/set/clear ----------------------------------
0006CBr 3               ; ( n b -- t? )
0006CBr 3  rr rr 04 74  def_word "tbit", "tbit", 0            ; nondestructive test bit
0006CFr 3  62 69 74     
0006D2r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_1     ; which bit
0006D5r 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2

0006D9r 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2
0006DBr 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0

0006E1r 3  20 rr rr         jsr BITWIND

0006F9r 3

0006FDr 3  62 69 74     
000700r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_1     ; which bit

00073Dr 3

000783r 3  rr rr 04 6E  def_word "nand", "nand", 0
000787r 3  61 6E 64

0007D2r 3

0007D2r 3               ; ( 0 -- $0000) | ( n -- $FFFF)  normalize boolean - change TOS to $0000 if false, $FFFF if TRUE
0007D2r 3  rr rr 04 62  def_word "bool", "normbool", 0

001AEAr 2               ;
001AEAr 2               ;-----------------------------------------------------------------------
001AEAr 2               ;                            BINARY LIBRARIES
001AEAr 2               ;
001AEAr 2               .include "bload.s"
001AEAr 3               ;
001AEAr 3               ;  BLOAD test code
001AEAr 3               ;
001AEAr 3               ;  ALL binary libraries loadable with 'bload' MUST be Forth words,
001AEAr 3               ;   (not jsr subroutines or jmp destinations), fully relocatable.  That means NO direct jmp/jsr's
001AEAr 3               ;   to internal labels.  Indirect jmps ($6C / $7C) to local locations can be used
001AEAr 3               ;   once the word is loaded, with smart code.   Branches will work fine.
001AEAr 3               ;
001AEAr 3               ;   jsr's and jmp's to core primitive labels (next, keeps, this, etc) or
001AEAr 3               ;   OS ROM ($E000 - $FFFF) locations will also work.
001AEAr 3               ;
001AEAr 3               ;   These words will be loaded into the RAM heap, so there CAN be self-modding
001AEAr 3               ;   sections that will help words to configure themselves.  Be creative!
001AEAr 3               ;
001AEAr 3               ;  Format below must be rigidly followed, with length of name followed
001AEAr 3               ;   by that many bytes for the name.  'bit #0' ($89 $00) or $22 $00 (undocumented)
001AEAr 3               ;   must be the first instruction in any native code word loaded past
001AEAr 3               ;   'exit'.  Termination must be $00 $00 for each word, with "END"
001AEAr 3               ;   to designate the end of a 'library'.
001AEAr 3               ;
001AEAr 3               ;    LIBRARY header in standard format (reachable from lib_jumptable entry)
001AEAr 3               ;
001AEAr 3               ;    LT_xxxx label, followed by 'sz' string, followed by 'C' or 'B' type
001AEAr 3               ;    ..and then calculated ROM address of start of first loadable word, bigendian.
001AEAr 3               ;
001AEAr 3               LT_BLOAD:
001AEAr 3  42 4C 4F 41  .byte "BLOAD"
001AEEr 3  44           
001AEFr 3  00           .byte 0
001AF0r 3  42           .byte "B"
001AF1r 3  BB           .byte >(bload0 + COPYSTART - RAMSTART)
001AF2r 3  F3           .byte <(bload0 + COPYSTART - RAMSTART)
001AF3r 3               bload0:
001AF3r 3               ;
001AF3r 3  06           .byte 6           ; length of name
001AF4r 3  42 4C 74 65  .byte "BLtest"
001AF8r 3  73 74        
001AFAr 3  22                .byte $22
001AFBr 3  00                .byte 0
001AFCr 3  48 A9 0D 20       WCRLF
001B00r 3  rr rr A9 0A  
001B04r 3  20 rr rr 68  
001B08r 3  A9 54             lda #'T'
001B0Ar 3  20 rr rr          jsr WRITE_CHAR
001B0Dr 3  A9 45             lda  #'E'
001B0Fr 3  20 rr rr          jsr WRITE_CHAR
001B12r 3  A9 53             lda  #'S'
001B14r 3  20 rr rr          jsr WRITE_CHAR
001B17r 3  A9 54             lda  #'T'
001B19r 3  20 rr rr          jsr WRITE_CHAR
001B1Cr 3  48 A9 0D 20       WCRLF
001B20r 3  rr rr A9 0A  
001B24r 3  20 rr rr 68  
001B28r 3  4C rr rr          jmp next
001B2Br 3  00 00        .byte 0, 0
001B2Dr 3  07           .byte 7           ; length of name
001B2Er 3  42 4C 74 65  .byte "BLtest2"
001B32r 3  73 74 32     
001B35r 3  22                .byte $22
001B36r 3  00                .byte 0
001B37r 3  48 A9 0D 20       WCRLF
001B3Br 3  rr rr A9 0A  
001B3Fr 3  20 rr rr 68  
001B43r 3  A9 54             lda #'T'
001B45r 3  20 rr rr          jsr WRITE_CHAR
001B48r 3  A9 45             lda #'E'
001B4Ar 3  20 rr rr          jsr WRITE_CHAR
001B4Dr 3  A9 53             lda #'S'
001B4Fr 3  20 rr rr          jsr WRITE_CHAR
001B52r 3  A9 54             lda #'T'
001B54r 3  20 rr rr          jsr WRITE_CHAR
001B57r 3  A9 59             lda #'Y'
001B59r 3  20 rr rr          jsr WRITE_CHAR
001B5Cr 3  48 A9 0D 20       WCRLF
001B60r 3  rr rr A9 0A  
001B64r 3  20 rr rr 68  
001B68r 3  4C rr rr          jmp next
001B6Br 3  00 00        .byte 0, 0
001B6Dr 3  45 4E 44     .byte "END"                  ; required to end library!
001B70r 3               bload0_end:

001B70r 2               ;
001B70r 2               ;  end of hyforth.s
001B70r 2               ;
001B70r 2               
001B70r 1               .include "wozmon.s"
001B70r 2               ;
001B70r 2               ;   Taken mostly from Steve Wozniak's Apple 1 Monitor for the 6502, or WOZMON
001B70r 2               ;
001B70r 2               .debuginfo
001B70r 2               .segment "BUFFERS"
000500r 2               IN:
000500r 2  xx xx xx xx                  .res            $100
000504r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000508r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000600r 2               ;
000600r 2               .segment "WOZMON"
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ; usually ends up at $FE00  (see os_rom_C02.cfg)
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ; WOZMON Entrypoint
000000r 2               MON_START:
000000r 2  D8                           cld                             ; Clear decimal arithmetic mode.
000001r 2  58                           cli                             ; Enable interrupts
000002r 2  64 rr                        stz             ZP_D_STATE      ; Byte mode, not DISAM mode
000004r 2  80 0B                        bra             @is_start
000006r 2               
000006r 2               @not_cr:
000006r 2  C9 08                        cmp             #$08            ; backspace
000008r 2  F0 0F                        beq             @is_backspace
00000Ar 2  C9 1B                        cmp             #$1B            ; escape
00000Cr 2  F0 03                        beq             @is_start
00000Er 2  C8                           iny                             ; Advance text index.
00000Fr 2  10 0B                        bpl             @get_next_char  ; Auto ESC if line longer than 127.
000011r 2               
000011r 2               @is_start:
000011r 2  20 rr rr                     PRINT_CRLF
000014r 2               
000014r 2               @get_line:
000014r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             WRITE_PROMPT
000017r 2  A0 01                        ldy             #1              ; Initialize text index.
000019r 2               
000019r 2               @is_backspace:
000019r 2  88                           dey                             ; Back up text index.
00001Ar 2  30 F8                        bmi             @get_line       ; Beyond start of line, reinitialize.
00001Cr 2               
00001Cr 2               @get_next_char:
00001Cr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             READ_CHAR
00001Fr 2  90 FB                        bcc             @get_next_char
000021r 2  99 rr rr                     sta             IN,y            ; Add to text buffer.
000024r 2  C9 0D                        cmp             #$0D
000026r 2  D0 DE                        bne             @not_cr
000028r 2  A0 FF                        ldy             #$FF            ; Reset text index.  Will iny shortly...
00002Ar 2  A9 00                        lda             #$00            ; For ZP_WM_XAM mode.
00002Cr 2  AA                           tax                             ; .X=0.
00002Dr 2               
00002Dr 2               @set_block:
00002Dr 2  0A                           asl
00002Er 2               
00002Er 2               @set_store:
00002Er 2  0A                           asl                             ; Leaves $7B if setting STOR mode.
00002Fr 2               
00002Fr 2               @set_mode:
00002Fr 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_WM_MODE      ; $00 = ZP_WM_XAM, $74 = STOR, $B8 = BLOK ZP_WM_XAM.
000031r 2               
000031r 2               @skip_delim:
000031r 2  C8                           iny                             ; Advance text index.
000032r 2               
000032r 2               @next_item:
000032r 2  B9 rr rr                     lda             IN,y            ; Get character.
000035r 2  C9 0D                        cmp             #$0D      ; CR?
000037r 2  F0 DB                        beq             @get_line       ; Yes, done with this line.
000039r 2  C9 2E                        cmp             #'.'
00003Br 2  90 F4                        bcc             @skip_delim     ; Skip delimiter.
00003Dr 2  F0 EE                        beq             @set_block      ; Set BLOCK ZP_WM_XAM mode.
00003Fr 2  C9 3A                        cmp             #':'
000041r 2  F0 EB                        beq             @set_store      ; Yes, set STOR mode.
000043r 2  C9 4D                        cmp             #'M'
000045r 2  10 0A                        bpl             @check_rtow
000047r 2  C9 4B                        cmp             #'K'
000049r 2  30 2B                        bmi             @not_tuvw
00004Br 2  49 4B                        eor             #'K'        ; 'K' resets DASTATE to 0
00004Dr 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_D_STATE
00004Fr 2  80 E0                        bra             @skip_delim
000051r 2               
000051r 2               @check_rtow:
000051r 2  C9 52                        cmp             #'R'
000053r 2  F0 5B                        beq             @run_prog
000055r 2  90 1F                        bcc             @not_tuvw
000057r 2  C9 58                        cmp             #'X'        ; R, S, T, U, V, or W
000059r 2  B0 1B                        bcs             @not_tuvw
00005Br 2  C9 53                        cmp             #'S'
00005Dr 2  D0 09                        bne             @not_spawn
00005Fr 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_WM_XAM
000061r 2  A4 rr                        ldy             ZP_WM_XAM + 1
000063r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             SPAWN_TASK
000066r 2  80 98                        bra             MON_START
000068r 2               
000068r 2               @not_spawn:
000068r 2  18                           clc
000069r 2  69 9C                        adc             #($F0 - 'T'); T=FFF0, U=FFF1, V=FFF2, W=FFF3
00006Br 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_WM_HVP       ;
00006Dr 2  A9 FF                        lda             #$FF            ;
00006Fr 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_WM_HVP + 1   ;
000071r 2  C8                           iny                             ; skip the mnemonic
000072r 2  80 2C                        bra             @not_hex_or_escape
000074r 2               
000074r 2               @bra_is_start:
000074r 2  80 9B                        bra             @is_start       ; unrecognized
000076r 2               
000076r 2               @not_tuvw:
000076r 2  84 rr                        sty             ZP_Y_SAVE       ; Save Y for comparison
000078r 2  86 rr                        stx             ZP_WM_HVP       ; $00 -> Low byte of HPV
00007Ar 2  86 rr                        stx             ZP_WM_HVP + 1   ; ...and High byte.
00007Cr 2               
00007Cr 2               @next_hex:
00007Cr 2  B9 rr rr                     lda             IN,y            ; Get character for hex test.
00007Fr 2  49 30                        eor             #'0'            ; Map digits to $0-9.
000081r 2  C9 0A                        cmp             #$0A            ; Digit?
000083r 2  90 06                        bcc             @is_digit       ; Yes.
000085r 2  69 88                        adc             #$88            ; Map letter "A"-"F" to $FA-FF.
000087r 2  C9 FA                        cmp             #$FA            ; Hex letter?
000089r 2  90 11                        bcc             @not_hex        ; No, character not hex.
00008Br 2               
00008Br 2               @is_digit:
00008Br 2  0A                           asl                             ; LSD to MSD of A.
00008Cr 2  0A                           asl
00008Dr 2  0A                           asl
00008Er 2  0A                           asl
00008Fr 2  A2 04                        ldx             #4              ; Shift count.
000091r 2               
000091r 2               @hex_shift:
000091r 2  0A                           asl                             ; Hex digit left, MSB to carry.
000092r 2  26 rr                        rol             ZP_WM_HVP       ; Rotate into LSD.
000094r 2  26 rr                        rol             ZP_WM_HVP + 1   ; Rotate into MSD's.
000096r 2  CA                           dex                             ; Done 4 shifts?
000097r 2  D0 F8                        bne             @hex_shift      ; No, loop.
000099r 2  C8                           iny                             ; Advance text index.
00009Ar 2  D0 E0                        bne             @next_hex       ; Always taken. Check next character for hex.
00009Cr 2               
00009Cr 2               @not_hex:
00009Cr 2  C4 rr                        cpy             ZP_Y_SAVE       ; Check if HPV empty (no hex digits).
00009Er 2  F0 D4                        beq             @bra_is_start   ; Yes, back to start.
0000A0r 2               
0000A0r 2               @not_hex_or_escape:
0000A0r 2  24 rr                        bit             ZP_WM_MODE      ; Test ZP_WM_MODE byte.
0000A2r 2  50 15                        bvc             @not_store      ; B6=0 is STOR, 1 is XAM and BLOCK XAM.
0000A4r 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_WM_HVP       ; LSD's of hex data.
0000A6r 2  92 rr                        sta             (ZP_WM_ST)      ; Store to current 'store index'.
0000A8r 2  E6 rr                        inc             ZP_WM_ST        ; Increment store index.
0000AAr 2  D0 86                        bne             @next_item      ; Get next item (no carry).
0000ACr 2  E6 rr                        inc             ZP_WM_ST + 1    ; Add carry to 'store index' high order.
0000AEr 2               
0000AEr 2               @to_next_item:
0000AEr 2  80 82                        bra             @next_item      ; Get next command item.
0000B0r 2               
0000B0r 2               @run_prog:
0000B0r 2  A9 rr                        lda             #>(MON_START-1)   ; push Wozmon entry point on stack
0000B2r 2  48                           pha
0000B3r 2  A9 rr                        lda             #<(MON_START-1)
0000B5r 2  48                           pha
0000B6r 2  6C rr rr                     jmp             (ZP_WM_XAM)       ; then jump to program
0000B9r 2               @not_store:
0000B9r 2  30 22                        bmi             @examine_next   ; B7 = 0 for XAM, 1 for BLOCK XAM.
0000BBr 2  A2 02                        ldx             #2              ; Byte count.
0000BDr 2               
0000BDr 2               @set_addr:
0000BDr 2  B5 rr                        lda             ZP_WM_HVP - 1,x ; Copy hex data to
0000BFr 2  95 rr                        sta             ZP_WM_ST - 1,x  ;   'store index'.
0000C1r 2  95 rr                        sta             ZP_WM_XAM - 1,x ; And to 'ZP_WM_XAM index'.
0000C3r 2  CA                           dex                             ; Next of 2 bytes.
0000C4r 2  D0 F7                        bne             @set_addr       ; Loop unless X = 0.
0000C6r 2               
0000C6r 2               @print_next_addr:
0000C6r 2  20 rr rr                     PRINT_CRLF
0000C9r 2  A5 rr 20 rr                  PRINT_BYTE      ZP_WM_XAM + 1, ZP_WM_XAM   ; Print 'examine index'
0000CDr 2  rr A5 rr 20  
0000D1r 2  rr rr        
0000D3r 2  A9 3A 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR      #':'                      ; ...+ COLON
0000D7r 2  rr           
0000D8r 2               
0000D8r 2               @print_data:
0000D8r 2  5A                           phy
0000D9r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             DISASM_WM       ; DISASM
0000DCr 2  7A                           ply
0000DDr 2               
0000DDr 2               @examine_next:
0000DDr 2  64 rr                        stz             ZP_WM_MODE      ; 0 -> ZP_WM_MODE (XAM mode).
0000DFr 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_WM_XAM
0000E1r 2  C5 rr                        cmp             ZP_WM_HVP       ; Compare 'examine index' to hex data.
0000E3r 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_WM_XAM + 1
0000E5r 2  E5 rr                        sbc             ZP_WM_HVP + 1
0000E7r 2  B0 C5                        bcs             @to_next_item   ; Not less, so no more data to output.
0000E9r 2  38                           sec
0000EAr 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_D_EXBYTES
0000ECr 2  65 rr                        adc             ZP_WM_XAM
0000EEr 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_WM_XAM
0000F0r 2  90 02                        bcc             :+
0000F2r 2  E6 rr                        inc             ZP_WM_XAM + 1
0000F4r 2               :
0000F4r 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_D_STATE      ; if disassembling, always print the address
0000F6r 2  D0 CE                        bne             @print_next_addr
0000F8r 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_WM_XAM       ; Check low-order 'examine index' byte
0000FAr 2  29 07                        and             #7              ; For MOD 8 = 0
0000FCr 2  F0 C8                        beq             @print_next_addr
0000FEr 2  80 D8                        bra             @print_data
000100r 2               
000100r 1               ;.include "mmu.s"
000100r 1               .include "shell.s"
000100r 2               .debuginfo
000100r 2               .segment "SHELL"
000000r 2               
000000r 2               SHELL_MAIN:
000000r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr     MON_START
000003r 2               
000003r 1               .include "sound.s"
000003r 2               .debuginfo
000003r 2               .segment "SOUND"
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ; usually ends up at $E400 (see os_rom_C02.cfg)
000000r 2               ;
000000r 2               ; zero out all YM-2151 registers $28-$FF
000000r 2               SOUND_INIT:
000000r 2  48                           pha
000001r 2  DA                           phx
000002r 2  A9 00                        lda         #0
000004r 2  A2 14                        ldx         #$14        ; turn off the clocks
000006r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
000009r 2  A2 28                        ldx         #$28
00000Br 2               
00000Br 2               @write_z:
00000Br 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
00000Er 2  B0 0D                        bcs         @error
000010r 2  E8                           inx
000011r 2  D0 F8                        bne         @write_z
000013r 2  A2 03                        ldx         #IRQ_NUMBER_ONBOARD_SOUND
000015r 2  A9 rr                        lda         #<SOUND_IRQ_HANDLER
000017r 2  A0 rr                        ldy         #>SOUND_IRQ_HANDLER
000019r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         IRQ_SET_VECTOR
00001Cr 2  18                           clc
00001Dr 2               
00001Dr 2               @error:
00001Dr 2  FA                           plx
00001Er 2  68                           pla
00001Fr 2  60                           rts
000020r 2               
000020r 2               
000020r 2               SOUND_IRQ_HANDLER:
000020r 2                               ; check which
000020r 2  40                           rti
000021r 2               
000021r 2               YMN0L = $A0
000021r 2               YMN0H = YMN0L + 1
000021r 2               YMN1L = YMN0L + 2
000021r 2               YMN1H = YMN0L + 3
000021r 2               
000021r 2               AZP0L = $B0
000021r 2               AZP0H = AZP0L + 1
000021r 2               YMTMP1 = AZP0L + 2
000021r 2               YMTMP2 = AZP0L + 3
000021r 2

000095r 2  49 55 49     
000098r 2               
000098r 2               SOUND_TEST:
000098r 2                               ; Set basic FM patch (simple sine-like sound)
000098r 2  A2 20                        ldx         #$20
00009Ar 2  A9 C7                        lda         #$C7
00009Cr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
00009Fr 2               
00009Fr 2  A2 80                        ldx         #$80
0000A1r 2  A9 1F                        lda         #$1F
0000A3r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
0000A6r 2

0000A8r 2  A9 0F                        lda         #$0F
0000AAr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
0000ADr 2

0000B4r 2

0000B6r 2  A9 1F                        lda         #$1F

0000BBr 2
