0000BDr 2  A9 0F                        lda         #$0F
0000BFr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
0000C2r 2               
0000C2r 2  A9 00                        lda         #$00    ; Channel 0
0000C4r 2  A2 rr                        ldx         #<GUITAR_PATCH
0000C6r 2  A0 rr                        ldy         #>GUITAR_PATCH
0000C8r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_LOADPATCH
0000CBr 2               
0000CBr 2  A9 01                        lda         #$01    ; Channel 1
0000CDr 2  A2 rr                        ldx         #<GUITAR_PATCH
0000CFr 2  A0 rr                        ldy         #>GUITAR_PATCH
0000D1r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_LOADPATCH
0000D4r 2               
0000D4r 2  A2 60                        ldx         #$60    ; Total Level (volume) for operator 1, Channel 0
0000D6r 2  A9 00                        lda         #$00    ; Max Volume
0000D8r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
0000DBr 2               
0000DBr 2  A2 61                        ldx         #$61    ; Total Level (volume) for operator 1, Channel 1
0000DDr 2  A9 00                        lda         #$00    ; Max Volume
0000DFr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
0000E2r 2               
0000E2r 2  A9 rr                        lda         #<SCOM_NOTES_0
0000E4r 2  85 A0                        sta         YMN0L
0000E6r 2  A9 rr                        lda         #>SCOM_NOTES_0
0000E8r 2  85 A1                        sta         YMN0H
0000EAr 2               
0000EAr 2  A9 rr                        lda         #<SCOM_NOTES_1
0000ECr 2  85 A2                        sta         YMN1L
0000EEr 2  A9 rr                        lda         #>SCOM_NOTES_1
0000F0r 2  85 A3                        sta         YMN1H
0000F2r 2               
0000F2r 2  A0 00                        ldy         #0          ; counter for channel 0
0000F4r 2               @play_loop:
0000F4r 2                               ; 0. set outer counter to 0
0000F4r 2                               ; 1. key off channel 0
0000F4r 2                               ; 1a.  key off on channel 1
0000F4r 2                               ; 2. set note to next note in channel 0 list
0000F4r 2                               ; 3. key on on channel 0
0000F4r 2                               ; 4. one delay
0000F4r 2                               ; 5. notes for rest of sequence on channel 1
0000F4r 2                               ; 5a.  reset counter to 0
0000F4r 2                               ; 5b.  key off on channel 1
0000F4r 2                               ; 5c.  set note on channel 1
0000F4r 2                               ; 5d.  key on on channel 1
0000F4r 2                               ; 5e.  one delay
0000F4r 2                               ; 5f.  increment counter
0000F4r 2                               ; 5g.  loop until 7 notes have been played on channel 1
0000F4r 2                               ; 6.  increment outer counter to play next start note on channel 0
0000F4r 2                               ; 7.  loop back to 1 until outer count reaches 8 (8 sequences have played)
0000F4r 2                               ; Key Off (silence previous note)
0000F4r 2               
0000F4r 2  A2 08                        ldx         #$08    ; Key On/Off register
0000F6r 2  A9 00                        lda         #$00    ; CH0 off
0000F8r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
0000FBr 2               
0000FBr 2  A2 08                        ldx         #$08    ; Key On/Off register
0000FDr 2  A9 01                        lda         #$01    ; CH1 off
0000FFr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
000102r 2               
000102r 2                               ; Set frequency (KC)
000102r 2  A2 28                        ldx         #$28    ; KC register for CH0
000104r 2  B1 A0                        lda         (YMN0L),y
000106r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
000109r 2               
000109r 2                               ; Key On
000109r 2  A2 08                        ldx         #$08    ; Key On/Off register
00010Br 2  A9 78                        lda         #$78    ; CH0 on
00010Dr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
000110r 2               
000110r 2                               ; Delay 8 64th notes in length
000110r 2  A9 08                        lda         #8
000112r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_DELAY_64
000115r 2               
000115r 2  5A                           phy
000116r 2  A0 00                        ldy         #0
000118r 2  80 07                        bra         @skip_ch1_off   ; already off at the start of the loop
00011Ar 2               
00011Ar 2               @next_ch1:
00011Ar 2  A2 08                        ldx         #$08    ; Key On/Off register
00011Cr 2  A9 01                        lda         #$01    ; CH1 off
00011Er 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
000121r 2               
000121r 2               @skip_ch1_off:
000121r 2                               ; Set frequency (KC)
000121r 2  A2 29                        ldx         #$29    ; KC register for CH1
000123r 2  B1 A2                        lda         (YMN1L),y
000125r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
000128r 2               
000128r 2                               ; Key On
000128r 2  A2 08                        ldx         #$08    ; Key On/Off register
00012Ar 2  A9 79                        lda         #$79    ; CH1 on
00012Cr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
00012Fr 2               
00012Fr 2                               ; Delay 8 64th notes in length
00012Fr 2  A9 08                        lda         #8
000131r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_DELAY_64
000134r 2               
000134r 2  C8                           iny
000135r 2  C0 07                        cpy         #7
000137r 2  D0 E1                        bne         @next_ch1
000139r 2               
000139r 2                               ; Next note on CH0
000139r 2  7A                           ply                 ; pull CH0 count
00013Ar 2  C8                           iny
00013Br 2  C0 08                        cpy         #8      ; 8 bars
00013Dr 2  D0 B5                        bne         @play_loop
00013Fr 2               
00013Fr 2                               ; Stop sound
00013Fr 2  A2 08                        ldx         #$08
000141r 2  A9 00                        lda         #$00
000143r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
000146r 2               
000146r 2  A2 08                        ldx         #$08
000148r 2  A9 01                        lda         #$01
00014Ar 2  4C rr rr                     jmp         YM_WRITE
00014Dr 2               
00014Dr 2               YM_TIMEOUT = 64
00014Dr 2               
00014Dr 2               ; Write value in A to YM-2151 register in X
00014Dr 2               YM_WRITE:
00014Dr 2  78                           sei
00014Er 2  5A                           phy
00014Fr 2  A0 40                        ldy         #YM_TIMEOUT
000151r 2               
000151r 2               @ym_wait1:
000151r 2  88                           dey
000152r 2  30 18                        bmi         @timeout
000154r 2  2C rr rr                     bit         YM_DATA
000157r 2  30 F8                        bmi         @ym_wait1
000159r 2  8E rr rr                     stx         YM_REG
00015Cr 2  A0 40                        ldy         #YM_TIMEOUT
00015Er 2               
00015Er 2               @ym_wait2:
00015Er 2  88                           dey
00015Fr 2  30 0B                        bmi         @timeout
000161r 2  2C rr rr                     bit         YM_DATA
000164r 2  30 F8                        bmi         @ym_wait2
000166r 2  8D rr rr                     sta         YM_DATA
000169r 2  18                           clc
00016Ar 2  80 01                        bra         @cleanup
00016Cr 2               
00016Cr 2               @timeout:
00016Cr 2  38                           sec
00016Dr 2               
00016Dr 2               @cleanup:
00016Dr 2  7A                           ply
00016Er 2  58                           cli
00016Fr 2  60                           rts
000170r 2               
000170r 2               YM_LOADPATCH:
000170r 2                               ; Make re-entrant safe by protecting tmp and pointer variables from interrupt
000170r 2  08                           php
000171r 2  78                           sei
000172r 2               
000172r 2                               ; and #$07 ; mask channel to range 0..7
000172r 2  86 B0                        stx AZP0L
000174r 2  84 B1                        sty AZP0H
000176r 2  18                           clc
000177r 2  69 20                        adc #$20 ; first byte of patch goes to YM:$20+channel
000179r 2  AA                           tax
00017Ar 2               
00017Ar 2  B2 B0                        lda (AZP0L)
00017Cr 2  29 3F                        and #$3F
00017Er 2               
00017Er 2                               ;sta ymtmp1
00017Er 2                               ;lda ymshadow,x
00017Er 2                               ;and #$C0 ; L+R bits for YM channel
00017Er 2                               ;ora ymtmp1 ; Add the patch byte without L+R
00017Er 2  09 C0                        ora #$C0;
000180r 2               
000180r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr YM_WRITE
000183r 2  B0 16                        bcs @fail
000185r 2  A0 00                        ldy #0
000187r 2  8A                           txa         ; YM_WRITE preserves X (YM register)
000188r 2                               ; Now skip over $28 and $30 by adding $10 to the register address.
000188r 2                               ; C guaranteed clear by successful ym_write
000188r 2  69 10                        adc #$10
00018Ar 2  AA                           tax         ; set up for loop
00018Br 2               @next:
00018Br 2  8A                           txa
00018Cr 2                               ; C guaranteed clear by successful YM_WRITE
00018Cr 2  69 08                        adc #$08
00018Er 2  B0 0E                        bcs @success
000190r 2  C8                           iny
000191r 2  AA                           tax
000192r 2  B1 B0                        lda (AZP0L),y
000194r 2  5A                           phy         ; YM_WRITE clobbers .Y
000195r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr YM_WRITE
000198r 2  7A                           ply
000199r 2  90 F0                        bcc @next
00019Br 2               @fail:
00019Br 2  28                           plp         ; restore interrupt flag
00019Cr 2  38                           sec
00019Dr 2  60                           rts         ; return C set as failed patch write.
00019Er 2               @success:
00019Er 2  28                           plp         ; restore interrupt flag
00019Fr 2  18                           clc
0001A0r 2  60                           rts
0001A1r 2               
0001A1r 2               ; YM_DELAY_64 - Delay subroutine for musical note lengths
0001A1r 2               ; A: number of 64th-note delays to wait (a 64th note @120 BPM = ~ 111,861 clock cycles @ 3.57955 MHz)
0001A1r 2               YM_DELAY_64:
0001A1r 2  DA                           phx
0001A2r 2  5A                           phy
0001A3r 2  85 B2                        sta         YMTMP1
0001A5r 2               
0001A5r 2               @far_outer:
0001A5r 2  A9 01                        lda         #1          ; 2 cycles
0001A7r 2  A2 4B                        ldx         #75
0001A9r 2  A0 8E                        ldy         #142
0001ABr 2  80 04                        bra         @inner
0001ADr 2               
0001ADr 2               @outer:
0001ADr 2  A2 00                        ldx         #0          ; 2 cycles
0001AFr 2               
0001AFr 2               @mid:
0001AFr 2  A0 00                        ldy         #0          ; 2 cycles
0001B1r 2               
0001B1r 2               @inner:
0001B1r 2  88                           dey                     ; 2 cycles
0001B2r 2  D0 FD                        bne         @inner      ; 3 cycles when branching, 2 when not
0001B4r 2  CA                           dex                     ; 2 cycles
0001B5r 2  D0 F8                        bne         @mid        ; 3 cycles when branching, 2 when not
0001B7r 2               
0001B7r 2                               ; Fine-tuning at end of inner loop
0001B7r 2  3A                           dec                     ; 2 cycles
0001B8r 2  D0 F3                        bne         @outer      ; 3 cycles when branching, 2 when not
0001BAr 2  C6 B2                        dec         YMTMP1      ; 3 cycles
0001BCr 2  D0 E7                        bne         @far_outer  ; 3 cycles when branching, 2 when not
0001BEr 2  7A                           ply
0001BFr 2  FA                           plx
0001C0r 2  60                           rts                     ; 6 cycles
0001C1r 2

000000r 2               RESET_VECTOR_START:
000000r 2  A9 00                    lda     #0                    ; ZERO the W register
000002r 2  8D F3 FF                 sta     W_REGISTER
000005r 2  D8                       cld                                                     ; We don't like decimal mode
000006r 2  8D F0 FF                 sta                 T_REGISTER                          ; Make sure task 0 is selected
000009r 2  85 00                    sta                 $00                                 ; Init RAM Bank selector
00000Br 2  85 01                    sta                 $01                                 ; Init ROM Bank selector

00000Fr 2  9A                       txs
000010r 2  A5 rr                    lda                 ZP_READ_PTR
000012r 2  85 rr                    sta                 ZP_WRITE_PTR           ; remove when tasks_init is used
000014r 2

000026r 2
